        end

      1 return M

******0     local entry = name_map[name]

******0       local icon = entry.icon or default_entry.icon
******0       local display = entry.name or name

******0       output = output .. (c > 1 and "" or " ") .. hl .. icon .. display .. " "
            else
******0       local hl = set_hl(default_entry.hl)

******0       output = output .. (c > 1 and "" or " ") .. hl .. default_entry.icon .. name .. " "
            end

******0   return output

          ---|fE

      1 return M

        --- Misc stats component.
        --- Shows neocodeium status, animate toggle, and noice/notify counts.

        ---@module "bars.components.misc_stats"

            [6] = "󱚠 ",
      1   },
      1   server_status = {
            [0] = "󰣺 ",
            [1] = "󰣻 ",
            [2] = "󰣽 ",
      1   },
        }

      1 local cached_result = ""
      1 local last_update = 0
      1 local THROTTLE_MS = 100

      1 local neocodeium = "       "

        --- Registers autocmds for neocodeium status updates.
        --- Call once during setup to enable live neocodeium status in the statusline.
        --- Safe to call multiple times (uses a named augroup).

          ---|fS

******0   local group = vim.api.nvim_create_augroup("BarsMiscStats", { clear = true })
******0   vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd("User", {

******0     pattern = {
              "NeoCodeiumLabelUpdated",
              "NeoCodeiumServer*",
              "NeoCodeium*Enabled",
              "NeoCodeium*Disabled",
              "NeoCodeiumCompletionCleared",
              "NeoCodeiumCompletionDisplayed",
              "NeoCodeiumBufEnabled",
              "NeoCodeiumBufDisabled",
              "NeoCodeiumEnabled",
              "NeoCodeiumDisabled",
            },

******0       if package.loaded["neocodeium"] and package.loaded["neocodeium"].get_status then
******0         local status, server_status = require("neocodeium").get_status()
******0         server_status = symbols.server_status[server_status]
******0         neocodeium = ev.data or server_status
              end
******0       vim.cmd("redrawstatus")
            end,
          })

          ---|fE
        end

        --- Returns the misc stats for the statusline.
        ---@param buffer integer
        ---@param window integer
        ---@return string
        M.value = function(buffer, window)
          ---|fS

      2   local now = vim.uv.now()

      2   if now - last_update < THROTTLE_MS and cached_result ~= "" then
      1     return cached_result
          end

      1   last_update = now

      1   local noice_count = package.loaded["noice"] and #require("noice.message.manager")._history or 0
      1   local pending = package.loaded["notify"] and require("notify").pending() or 0
      1   local history = package.loaded["notify"]
      1       and #(require("notify").history({ include_hidden = true }) or {})
      1     or 0

      2   local animate = vim.b.minianimate_disable == true and "   " or "  "

      1   local pending_str = pending > 0 and " (" .. tostring(pending) .. ")" or ""

******0   cached_result = neocodeium
      1     .. animate
      1     .. tostring(noice_count)
******0     .. ":"
      1     .. tostring(history)
      1     .. pending_str

      1   return cached_result

          ---|fE
        end

      1 return M

        --- Map Neovim highlight group to tmTheme scopes
        --- Returns comma-separated list of scopes for better coverage
        --- @param group_name string Neovim highlight group name
        --- @return string|nil Comma-separated tmTheme scope names
        local function map_hl_to_scopes(group_name)
   7410   local scope_map = {
            -- Text and variables

            Identifier = "variable.other.readwrite, punctuation.definition.variable",

            SpecialComment = "comment.block.documentation",

            Number = "constant.numeric",

            Label = "keyword.other.label",

            PreCondit = "keyword.control.conditional.preprocessor",

            StorageClass = "storage.modifier",

            Typedef = "storage.type.typedef",

            -- Special
            Special = "constant.other",
            SpecialChar = "constant.character.escape",

            Delimiter = "punctuation.separator, punctuation.terminator",

            Error = "invalid.illegal",
            ErrorMsg = "invalid.illegal, message.error",
            WarningMsg = "markup.warning, message.warning",

            -- Markup

            Underlined = "markup.underline",
            Bold = "markup.bold",
            Italic = "markup.italic",
            Todo = "markup.todo, keyword.other.todo",

   7410   return scope_map[group_name]

        --- Generate tmTheme XML for bat
        --- @param colorscheme_name string Name of the colorscheme
        --- @return string XML content in tmTheme format
      1 function M.generate_tmtheme(colorscheme_name)

     21   local normal = get_highlight("Normal")

        --- Export colorscheme to bat theme format in a new buffer
        --- @param opts table Options { colorscheme?: string, filename?: string }
      1 function M.export(opts)

     10   local colorscheme_name = opts.colorscheme or vim.g.colors_name or "nvim-theme"

      8   local bufnr = vim.api.nvim_create_buf(false, true)

          -- Set buffer options
     16   vim.bo[bufnr].buftype = "nofile"
     16   vim.bo[bufnr].bufhidden = "hide"
     16   vim.bo[bufnr].swapfile = false
     16   vim.bo[bufnr].filetype = "xml"

          -- Set buffer name
      8   local filename = opts.filename or (colorscheme_name .. ".tmTheme")
      8   vim.api.nvim_buf_set_name(bufnr, filename)

          -- Split the content into lines
      8   local lines = vim.split(xml_content, "\n")

          -- Set buffer content
      8   vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(bufnr, 0, -1, false, lines)

          -- Open buffer in a new window
      8   vim.cmd("split")
      8   vim.api.nvim_win_set_buf(0, bufnr)

     16   vim.notify(
     16     string.format(
      8       "Exported colorscheme '%s' to bat theme format (%d lines)",
      8       colorscheme_name,
      8       #lines
******0     ),
      9     vim.log.levels.INFO
      8   )

      8   return bufnr
        end

        -- Function to toggle a character before comments and trailing whitespace
        local function toggle_last_char(char)

******0   local line_num = vim.fn.line(".")
******0   local line = vim.fn.getline(line_num):gsub("%s+$", "") -- Remove trailing whitespace

          -- Get the comment string for current buffer
******0   local commentstring = vim.bo.commentstring

******0   local comment_marker = commentstring:match("^(.-)%%s")

******0   local insert_pos = comment_start or (#line + 1)

          -- Check if the character is already present at the insert position

******0   if line:sub(insert_pos - 1, insert_pos - 1) == char then

******0     new_line = line:sub(1, insert_pos - 2) .. line:sub(insert_pos)
          else

******0     new_line = line:sub(1, insert_pos - 1) .. char .. line:sub(insert_pos)
          end

          -- Set the modified line back to the buffer
******0   vim.fn.setline(line_num, new_line)
        end

        -- Main QuickClose logic
      1 function M.quick_close()
      6   local current_win = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()

          -- Case 1: If current window is floating, just close it
     12   if is_floating_window() then
      1     vim.api.nvim_win_close(current_win, false)
      1     return
          end

      5   local split_count = get_split_count()

          -- Case 2: Window has splits
      5   if split_count > 1 then
            -- Check if current window is a special split
      6     if is_special_split(current_win) then
      1       vim.api.nvim_win_close(current_win, false)
      1       return
            end

            -- Check if any other window is a special split
      2     local special_win = find_special_split_window()
      2     if special_win then
      1       vim.api.nvim_win_close(special_win, false)
      1       return
            end

            -- No special splits found, close current window
      1     vim.api.nvim_win_close(current_win, false)
      1     return
          end

          -- Case 3: Last window - delete buffer instead of closing window
      2   local buf_count = count_listed_buffers()

      2   local bufnr = vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
      2   if buf_count > 1 then
            -- Delete the buffer but keep the window
      2     vim.schedule(function()
      2       local ok, err = pcall(vim.cmd.bdelete)
      1       if not ok then
******0         vim.notify("Error deleting buffer: " .. err, vim.log.levels.ERROR)
              end
            end)
          else
            -- Last buffer and last window - show Alpha startup screen
      3     if vim.bo[bufnr].filetype == "Alpha" then
******0       vim.cmd("q")
            else
      1       local alpha_ok, _ = pcall(vim.cmd, "Alpha")
      1       if not alpha_ok then
                -- Fallback if Alpha is not available
      1         vim.cmd("enew")
              end
            end
          end
        end

        local yank_path = function()
      6   local path = vim.fn.expand('%:p')
      6   vim.fn.setreg('+', path)
      6   local home = vim.fn.getenv('HOME')
      6   local display_path = path
      6   if type(home) == "string" and home ~= "" then
     10     display_path = path:gsub(vim.pesc(home), '~')
          end
      7   vim.notify('`' .. display_path .. '` yanked to "+"', vim.log.levels.INFO)
        end

        local yank_cwd = function()
          local project_root
      3   local ok, project_nvim = pcall(require, 'project_nvim')
      3   if ok and project_nvim.get_project_root then
      6     project_root = project_nvim.get_project_root()
          end

          -- Fallback to vim.fn.getcwd() if project_nvim not available
      3   if not project_root or project_root == '' then
      6     project_root = vim.fn.getcwd()
          end

      3   vim.fn.setreg('+', project_root)
      3   vim.notify('`' .. project_root .. '` yanked to "+"', vim.log.levels.INFO)
        end

        local function trash_file()
      2   local fname = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_name(0)

      2   if fname == '' then
      1     vim.notify('Cannot trash: buffer has no associated file', vim.log.levels.WARN)
      1     return
          end

          -- Save file first if modified
      2   if vim.bo.modified then
******0     vim.cmd.update({ bang = true })
          end

      1   local basename = vim.fn.expand('%:t')

      2   local res = vim.fn.jobstart({ 'trash-put', fname })
      1   if res > 0 then
      2     vim.cmd.bwipeout()
      2     vim.notify('"' .. basename .. '" sent to trash', vim.log.levels.INFO)
          else
******0     vim.notify('Could not delete file: ' .. fname, vim.log.levels.ERROR)
          end
        end
      1 return {
      1   trash_file = trash_file,
      1   yank_path = yank_path,

        ---|fS "doc: Type definitions"

        ---@alias beacon.color
        ---| string Hwx code: #RRGGBB
        ---| number Decimal value: 12345678
        ---| number[] RGB: { R, G, B }
        ---| fun(): ( string | number | number[] ) Dynamic as value.

        --- Configuration for a beacon.
        ---@class beacon.config
        ---
        ---@field set_keymap? boolean Should `<leader><leader>` be remapped?
        ---@field on_motions? table<string, beacon.instance.config> Configuration for beacons used as indicators for motions.
        ---
        ---@field default beacon.instance.config Default config.

        --- Configuration for a beacon instance.
        ---@class beacon.instance.config
        ---
        ---@field from? beacon.color Start color.
        ---@field to? beacon.color End color.
        ---
        ---@field steps? integer Gradient steps.
        ---@field interval? integer Delay between each animation frame.

        --- Configuration for a beacon instance.
        ---@class beacon.instance.config

        --- Creates a new beacon.
        ---@class beacon.instance
        ---
        ---@field ns integer Anonymous namespace.
        ---
        ---@field window integer Window where the beacon should be shown(default is current window).
        ---@field buffer integer Buffer being shown in window.
        ---
        ---@field pos [ integer, integer ] A position(default is cursor position).
        ---
        ---@field from_color beacon.color Start color.
        ---@field to_color beacon.color End color.
        ---@field steps integer Number of steps the beacon will have.
        ---@field interval integer Interval between each draw cycle(in milliseconds).
        ---
        ---@field colors string[] Gradient highlight groups.
        ---@field step integer Current step.
        ---@field timer table Timer.
        ---
        ---@field __index beacon.instance
        ---
        ---@field __gradient fun(self: beacon.instance): string[] Creates the gradient for the beacon.
        ---@field __list_render fun(self: beacon.instance): nil Render function for `list` mode.
        ---@field __nolist_render fun(self: beacon.instance): nil Render function for normal mode.
        ---
        ---@field update fun(self: beacon.instance, window?: integer, config?: beacon.instance.config): nil Updates beacon state.
        ---@field pos fun(self: beacon.instance, pos: [ integer, integer ]): nil Updates beacon position.
        ---@field render fun(self: beacon.instance): nil Renders current frame.
        ---@field start fun(self: beacon.instance): nil Starts the beacon.
        ---@field stop fun(self: beacon.instance): nil Stops the beacon.

        ---|fE

        ---@param r integer
        ---@param g integer
        ---@param b integer

        local function get_fg(r, g, b)
          ---|fS

          local fg

******0   if package.loaded["components.highlights"] then
******0     local highlights = require("components.highlights")

******0     local this = highlights.srgb_to_oklab({
              r = r,
              g = g,
              b = b,
            })

            local _bg =
******0       highlights.srgb_to_oklab(highlights.get_property("bg", { "Normal" }, "#CDD6F4", "#1E1E2E"))

******0     if this.L >= _bg.L then
******0       return highlights.rgb_to_hex(highlights.oklab_to_srgb(_bg))
            else
******0       return highlights.rgb_to_hex(_fg)
            end
          else

******0     if brightness > 128 then
******0       fg = normal.bg or "#1E1E2E"

******0   return fg

        ---@param buffer integer

        local function default_hl(buffer, str, style, lnum, index)
          ---|fS

******0   if style == "virt_text" then
******0     local faded_bg = vim.api.nvim_get_hl(0, { name = "FadedBg" }).bg

******0     return {
              name = string.format("HL%d%d%d", buffer, lnum, index),
              value = { fg = str, bg = faded_bg },
            }
          else
******0     local __R, __G, __B = string.match(str, "#(%x%x?)(%x%x?)(%x%x?)")

******0     local R = math.max(math.min(255, tonumber(__R, 16)), 0)
******0     local G = math.max(math.min(255, tonumber(__G, 16)), 0)
******0     local B = math.max(math.min(255, tonumber(__B, 16)), 0)

******0     return {
              name = string.format("HL%d%d%d", buffer, lnum, index),
******0       value = {

              },
            }
          end

          ---|fE
        end

        --[[ Colors `lnum` numbered line in `buffer`. ]]
        ---@param buffer integer
        ---@param lnum integer 0-indexed.
        color.color_line = function(buffer, lnum)
          ---|fS

          ---@type string line text.
******0   local text = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_lines(buffer, lnum, lnum + 1, false)[1] or ""

          ---@type string[] Pattern names.
******0   local pattern_keys = vim.tbl_keys(color.config.patterns)
******0   table.sort(pattern_keys)

******0   local ID = 0

          local function color_pattern_ranges(key)
            ---@type color.config.pattern
******0     local pattern = color.config.patterns[key]
******0     if not pattern then
******0       return {}
            end

******0     local style = evaluate(pattern.color_style or color.config.color_style, buffer)
******0     local hl = pattern.hl or default_hl
******0     local render = pattern.render or default_render

******0     local regex = vim.regex(pattern.pattern or "")
******0     local current_column = 0

******0     while current_column < #text do
******0       local col_start, col_end = regex:match_line(buffer, lnum, current_column)
******0       if not col_start or not col_end then
******0         return
              end

******0       col_start = current_column + col_start
******0       col_end = current_column + col_end

******0       local match = string.sub(text, col_start + 1, col_end)
******0       local found_hl, group = pcall(hl, buffer, match, style, lnum, ID)

******0       if found_hl then
******0         pcall(vim.api.nvim_set_hl, 0, group.name, group.value)
******0         pcall(render, buffer, group.name, style, lnum, col_start, col_end)
              end

******0       current_column = col_end
******0       ID = ID + 1
            end
          end

          -- Clear the current line of `old decorations`.
******0   vim.api.nvim_buf_clear_namespace(buffer, color.ns, lnum, lnum + 1)

******0   for _, key in ipairs(pattern_keys) do
******0     color_pattern_ranges(key)
          end

          ---|fE
        end

==============================================================================
lua/components/diagnostics.lua

        --[[
        *Fancy* diagnostics for `Neovim`.

        ## Usage

        ```lua
        require("diagnostics").setup();
        ```

        You can then hit `D` to show diagnostics for the current line.

        ## Highlight groups

        This script uses highlight groups provided by [highlights.lua](https://github.com/OXY2DEV/nvim/blob/main/lua/scripts/highlights.lua).

        The used groups are,
        - `FancyDiagnostic`, Default group for diagnostics.
        - `FancyDiagnosticIcon`, Default group for the icons of diagnostics.
        - `FancyDiagnosticInfo`, Group for `information` diagnostics.
        - `FancyDiagnosticInfoIcon`, Group for the icons of `information` diagnostics.
        - `FancyDiagnosticHint`, Group for `hint` diagnostics.
        - `FancyDiagnosticHintIcon`, Group for the icons of `hint` diagnostics.
        - `FancyDiagnosticWarn`, Group for `warning` diagnostics.
        - `FancyDiagnosticWarnIcon`, Group for the icons of `warning` diagnostics.
        - `FancyDiagnosticError`, Group for `error` diagnostics.
        - `FancyDiagnosticErrorIcon`, Group for the icons of `error` diagnostics.

        ## Dependencies

        - [beacon.lua](https://github.com/OXY2DEV/nvim/blob/main/lua/scripts/beacon.lua), For highlighting cursor.
        - [highlights.lua](https://github.com/OXY2DEV/nvim/blob/main/lua/scripts/highlights.lua), For highlight groups.
        - [types/diagnostics.lua](https://github.com/OXY2DEV/nvim/blob/main/lua/scripts/types/diagnostics.lua), For type definition.

        ## Configuration

        Use `setup()` for setting configuration. See [types/diagnostics.lua#diagnostics.config](https://github.com/OXY2DEV/nvim/blob/main/lua/scripts/types/diagnostics.lua#L3-L19) for the type definition.
        ]]
      3 local diagnostics = {};

        --[[
        *Dynamic* highlights for `Neovim` to match the current `colorscheme`.

        Usage,

        ```lua
        require("highlights").setup();
        ```
        ]]
      5 local highlights = {}

        --- Clamps a value between 0 & 255.
        ---@param c integer
        ---@return integer
        local function clamp(c)
      6   return math.min(math.max(0, math.floor(c)), 255)
        end

        --[[ Turns given color into **RGB** color value. ]]
        ---@param input string | number
        ---@return config.hl.rgb
        ---@return boolean invalid_value Was the given value invalid?
        highlights.rgb = function(input)
          ---|fS

******0   local lookup = vim.api.nvim_get_color_map()
          local hex

******0   if type(input) == "string" and lookup[input] then
******0     hex = string.format("#%06x", lookup[input])
******0   elseif type(input) == "number" then
******0     hex = string.format("#%06x", input)
          else
******0     hex = type(input) == "string" and input or "#FFFFFF"
          end

******0   return {
            r = tonumber(string.sub(hex, 2, 3), 16),
            g = tonumber(string.sub(hex, 4, 5), 16),
            b = tonumber(string.sub(hex, 6, 7), 16),
          },
******0     type(input) ~= "string" and type(input) ~= "number"

          ---|fE
        end

        --[[ Simple RGB color mixer. ]]
        ---@param c1 config.hl.rgb | config.hl.Lab
        ---@param c2 config.hl.rgb | config.hl.Lab
        ---@param p1 number
        ---@param p2 number
        ---@return config.hl.rgb | config.hl.Lab
        highlights.mix = function(c1, c2, p1, p2)
          ---|fS

      1   local out = {}

      4   for k, v in pairs(c1) do
      3     if c2[k] then
      3       out[k] = (v * p1) + (c2[k] * p2)
            else
******0       out[k] = v
            end
          end

      1   return out

          ---|fE
        end

        --[[ `RGB` to `hex color code` converter. ]]
        ---@param color config.hl.rgb
        ---@return string
        highlights.rgb_to_hex = function(color)
      4   return string.format("#%02x%02x%02x", clamp(color.r), clamp(color.g), clamp(color.b))
        end

        ---|fS "chunk: sRGB <-> Oklab"

        --[[
        `sRGB` -> `Oklab` conversion.

        Source: https://bottosson.github.io/posts/oklab/#converting-from-linear-srgb-to-oklab
        License: https://bottosson.github.io/misc/License.txt
        ]]
        ---@param c config.hl.rgb
        ---@return config.hl.Lab
        highlights.srgb_to_oklab = function(c)
      4   local l = 0.4122214708 * c.r + 0.5363325363 * c.g + 0.0514459929 * c.b
      4   local m = 0.2119034982 * c.r + 0.6806995451 * c.g + 0.1073969566 * c.b
      4   local s = 0.0883024619 * c.r + 0.2817188376 * c.g + 0.6299787005 * c.b

      4   local l_ = math.pow(l, 1 / 3)
      4   local m_ = math.pow(m, 1 / 3)
      4   local s_ = math.pow(s, 1 / 3)

      4   return {
      4     L = 0.2104542553 * l_ + 0.7936177850 * m_ - 0.0040720468 * s_,
      4     a = 1.9779984951 * l_ - 2.4285922050 * m_ + 0.4505937099 * s_,
      4     b = 0.0259040371 * l_ + 0.7827717662 * m_ - 0.8086757660 * s_,
      4   }
        end

        --[[
        `Oklab` -> `sRGB` conversion.

        Source: https://bottosson.github.io/posts/oklab/#converting-from-linear-srgb-to-oklab
        License: https://bottosson.github.io/misc/License.txt
        ]]
        highlights.oklab_to_srgb = function(c)
      1   local l_ = c.L + 0.3963377774 * c.a + 0.2158037573 * c.b
      1   local m_ = c.L - 0.1055613458 * c.a - 0.0638541728 * c.b
      1   local s_ = c.L - 0.0894841775 * c.a - 1.2914855480 * c.b

      1   local l = l_ * l_ * l_
      1   local m = m_ * m_ * m_
      1   local s = s_ * s_ * s_

      1   return {
      2     r = clamp(4.0767416621 * l - 3.3077115913 * m + 0.2309699292 * s),
      2     g = clamp(-1.2684380046 * l + 2.6097574011 * m - 0.3413193965 * s),
      2     b = clamp(-0.0041960863 * l - 0.7034186147 * m + 1.7076147010 * s),
      1   }
        end

        --[[ Gets `property` from a list of `highlight group`s. ]]

        ---@class inspect.stringify.opts Options for `tree.stringify()`.
        ---
        ---@field named? boolean Only show **named TSNodes**?
        ---
        ---@field depth? integer Current parsing depth. **USED INTERNALLY!**
        ---
        ---@field injection_maps? table<string, inspect.stringify.injection> Mapping of `TSNode`s ID to injected `TSTree`s.
        ---@field injection_depth? integer Current injection depth.. **USED INTERNALLY!**
        ---
        ---@field language? string Node language name.


        ---@class inspect.stringify.data Data of a `TSNode` given by `tree.stringify()`.
        ---
        ---@field depth integer Node depth.
        ---@field injection_depth? integer Injection depth.
        ---
        ---@field language? string Language of a `TSNode`.
        ---@field injection? inspect.stringify.injection Injection for a `TSNode`.
        ---
        ---@field kind string Node `type`.
        ---@field id string Unique ID(a **string**, not an 'integer').
        ---@field range integer[] Node range as `{ row_start, col_start, row_end, col_end }`.
        ---@field height integer Number of lines a `TSNode` covers in the inspector window.
        ---
        ---@field node TSNode
        ---
        ---@field missing boolean Is it a *missing* node?
        ---@field named boolean Is it a *named* node?
        ---@field extra boolean Is it a *extra* node?
        ---@field has_error boolean Does the `TSNode` contain any *errors*?


        ---@class inspect.stringify.injection Object representing an injected `TSTree`.
        ---
        ---@field language string Language of the injected `TSTree`.
        ---@field root TSNode The injected `TSTree`.


        ---@class inspect.config Configuration for the inspector.
        ---
        ---@field indent string Text used for *indentation* of the `TSTree`.
        ---@field named_only boolean Should only *named* nodes be shown by default?
        ---
        ---@field named inspect.config.opts Configuration for *named* `TSNode`s.
        ---@field anonymous inspect.config.opts Configuration for *anonymous* `TSNode`s.
        ---
        ---@field injections inspect.config.opts Configuration for *injected* `TSTree`s.

        ---@field default table

        	end

        end

        --[[ Gets a single value from a `list` of values. ]]
        ---@param list any
        ---@param index any
        ---@return any
        local function from_list (list, index)
        	---|fS

******0 	local _list = eval(list);

******0 	if type(_list) ~= "table" then
******0 		return _list;
******0 	elseif #_list == 0 then
******0 		return;
        	end

******0 	return (type(index) == "number" and _list[index] ~= nil) and _list[index] or _list[#_list];

        	---|fE
        end

        --[[ Gets the value of `key` from `map`. ]]

******0 		return;

        --[[
        A customisable version of `:InspectTree` with,

        * Custom `TSNode` icons & highlights.
        * `gd` support.
        * Toggle between `named only` & `all` nodes.
        * Injection highlighting.
        * Debounced `TSNode` range highlighter.

        ## Usage:

        ```lua
        -- Creates the `:Tree` command
        require("tree").setup();

        -- Creates a new inspector for the current window
        require("tree").new_inspector(0);
        ```

        ]]

        ---@type integer Namespace used by this script.
      4 tree.ns = vim.api.nvim_create_namespace("inspect");

        ---@type inspect.config User configuration.
      4 tree.config = {
        	indent = "\t";
        	named_only = false,

      4 	named = require("components.tree_maps").named,
      4 	anonymous = require("components.tree_maps").anon,

        --[[ Maps `TSNode`s in the `parser` to the `injected TSTrees` they contain. ]]
        ---@param parser vim.treesitter.LanguageTree
        ---@return table<string, inspect.stringify.injection>
        tree.injection_map = function (parser)
        	---|fS

        	--[[ Checks if `b` is inside `a`. ]]
        	---@param a integer[]
        	---@param b integer[]
        	---@return boolean
        	local function within (a, b)
        		---|fS

******0 		local root_row_start = a[1];
******0 		local child_row_start = b[1];

******0 		local root_col_start = a[2];
******0 		local child_col_start = b[2];

******0 		local root_row_end = a[3];
******0 		local child_row_end = b[3];

******0 		local root_col_end = a[4];
******0 		local child_col_end = b[4];

******0 		if child_row_start < root_row_start then
        			-- Child node starts before `root`.
******0 			return false;
******0 		elseif child_row_end > root_row_end then
        			-- Child node ends after `root`.
******0 			return false;
******0 		elseif child_row_start == root_row_start and child_col_start < root_col_start then
        			-- Child node's column start before `root`.
******0 			return false;
******0 		elseif child_row_end == root_row_end and child_col_end > root_col_end then
        			-- Child node's column ends after `root`.
******0 			return false;
        		end

******0 		return true;

        		---|fE
        	end

******0 	local map = {};

******0 	parser:for_each_tree(function (TSTree, LanguageTree)
******0 		local main_root = TSTree:root();
******0 		local main_range = { main_root:range() };

        		--[[
        			Iterate over each `Language`'s trees and find the
        			corresponding `TSNode` in the **main tree** that
        			contains the `root` of the injected language.
        		]]
******0 		for _, childLanguage in pairs(LanguageTree:children()) do
******0 			for _, childTSTree in pairs(childLanguage:trees()) do
******0 				local sub_root = childTSTree:root();
******0 				local sub_range = { sub_root:range() };

******0 				if within(main_range, sub_range) then
******0 					local container = main_root:named_descendant_for_range(unpack(sub_range));

******0 					if container then
******0 						map[container:id()] = {
        							language = childLanguage:lang(),
******0 							root = sub_root
******0 						};
        					end
        				end
        			end
        		end
        	end);

******0 	return map;

        	---|fE
        end

        --[[ Turns `TSNode` objects into `query` strings. ]]
        ---@param node TSNode
        ---@param opts inspect.stringify.opts
        ---@return string[]
        ---@return inspect.stringify.data[]
        tree.stringify = function (node, opts)
        	---|fS

        	local function escape_str (str)
******0 		local escaped = string.gsub(str, "\\", "\\\\");

******0 		return escaped;

******0 				range[2],

******0 				range[3],

******0 		);

        	---|fE

        	---|fS "chunk: Injection"

        --[[ An instance of the `inspector`. ]]
        ---@class inspect.instance
        ---
        ---@field __index inspect.instance
        ---
        ---@field source integer The `buffer` this inspector is attached to.
        ---@field config inspect.config Configuration used by this inspector.
        ---
        ---@field buffer integer The `buffer` containing the viewer.
        ---@field window integer The `window` showing the `buffer`.
        ---
        ---
        ---@field __prepare fun(self: inspect.instance): nil Preparation steps(e.g. set `config`, `buffer`, `filetype` etc.)
        ---@field __hide_node fun(self: inspect.instance, node: TSNode): nil Hides/Conceals `node`.
        ---
        ---@field __named fun(self: inspect.instance, node: TSNode): nil Handler for *named* TSNodes.
        ---@field __anon fun(self: inspect.instance, node: TSNode): nil Handler for *anonymous* TSNodes.
        ---
        ---@field __injection fun(self: inspect.instance, lnum: integer, data: inspect.stringify.data): nil Handler for *injected* TSTrees.
        ---
        ---
        ---@field close fun(self: inspect.instance): nil Closes the inspector window.
        ---@field change_mode fun(self: inspect.instance): nil Changes between showing `named only` and `all` TSNodes.
        ---
        ---@field highlight_node fun(self: inspect.instance): nil Highlights the `TSNode` under cursor in the `source` buffer.
        ---@field goto_node fun(self: inspect.instance): nil Goes to the `TSNode` in the `source` buffer.
        ---
        ---@field open fun(self: inspect.instance): nil Opens the inspector window.
        ---
        ---@field set_keymaps fun(self: inspect.instance): nil Sets `keymaps` for the inspector `buffer`.
        ---@field set_autocmds fun(self: inspect.instance): nil Creates `autocmds` for the inspector `window`.

      4 function instance:change_mode ()
        	---|fS

******0 	local cursor = vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(self.window);
******0 	local old_data = self.__data[cursor[1]];

******0 	self.config.named_only = not self.config.named_only;
******0 	self:render();

******0 	for d, line_data in ipairs(self.__data) do
******0 		if line_data.node:equal(old_data.node) then
******0 			pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_set_cursor, self.window, { d, cursor[2] });
******0 			break;
        		end
        	end

        	---|fE
        end

      4 function instance:highlight_node ()
        	---|fS

******0 	vim.api.nvim_buf_clear_namespace(self.source, tree.ns, 0, -1);

******0 	local cursor = vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(self.window);
******0 	if not self.__data[cursor[1]] then return; end

******0 	local range = self.__data[cursor[1]].range;

******0 	vim.api.nvim_buf_set_extmark(self.source, tree.ns, range[1], range[2], {
        		end_row = range[3],
        		end_col = range[4],

******0 		hl_group = "Visual"
******0 	});

        	---|fE
        end

      4 function instance:goto_node ()
        	---|fS

******0 	local cursor = vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(self.window);
******0 	if not self.__data[cursor[1]] then return; end

******0 	local source_wins = vim.fn.win_findbuf(self.source);
******0 	if not source_wins[1] then return; end

******0 	local range = self.__data[cursor[1]].range;

******0 	pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_set_cursor, source_wins[1], { range[1] + 1, range[2] });
******0 	pcall(vim.api.nvim_set_current_win, source_wins[1]);

        	---|fE
        end

      4 function instance:render ()
        	---|fS

******0 	local parser = vim.treesitter.get_parser(self.source or 0, nil, {});
******0 	if not parser then return; end

******0 	vim.bo[self.buffer].modifiable = true;

******0 	local TSTree = parser:parse(true)[1];
******0 	local lines, data = tree.stringify(
******0 		TSTree:root(),
        		{
        			language = parser:lang(),
        			injection_maps = tree.injection_map(parser),

******0 			named = self.config.named_only
        		}
******0 	);

        	-- Temporarily store the `tree data`.
******0 	self.__data = data;

******0 	vim.api.nvim_buf_clear_namespace(self.buffer, tree.ns, 0, -1);
******0 	vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(self.buffer, 0, -1, false, lines);

******0 	vim.bo[self.buffer].modifiable = false;

        	-------------------------------------------------------------------------

        	---|fS "chunk: Tree-sitter highlighting"

******0 	local inspect_parser = vim.treesitter.get_parser(self.buffer, nil, {});
******0 	if not inspect_parser then return; end

******0 	local inspect_tree = inspect_parser:parse(true)[1];
******0 	local inspect_root = inspect_tree:root();

******0 	local query = vim.treesitter.query.parse("query", [[
        		[
        			"("
        			")"
        		] @paren

        		(named_node) @named
        		(anonymous_node) @anon
******0 	]]);

******0 	for id, node, _, _ in query:iter_captures(inspect_root, self.buffer) do
******0 		local name = query.captures[id];

******0 		if name == "paren" then
******0 			self:__hide_node(node);
******0 		elseif name == "named" and self.config.named then
******0 			self:__named(node);
******0 		elseif name == "anon" and self.config.anonymous then
******0 			self:__anon(node);
        		end
        	end

        	---|fE

******0 	for lnum, line_data in ipairs(data) do
******0 		self:__injection(lnum, line_data);
        	end

        	---|fE
        end

      4 function instance:open ()
        	---|fS

******0 	self:__prepare();

******0 	if not pcall(vim.treesitter.get_parser, self.source) then
******0 		self:close();
******0 		return;
        	end

******0 	self:render();

******0 	if self.window and vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(self.window) then
******0 		pcall(vim.api.nvim_set_current_win, self.window);
        	else
******0 		self.window = vim.api.nvim_open_win(self.buffer, true, {
******0 			split = "below"
******0 		});
        	end

******0 	vim.wo[self.window].conceallevel = 3;
******0 	vim.wo[self.window].concealcursor = "nvc";

******0 	vim.wo[self.window].statuscolumn = " ";

******0 	self:highlight_node();

        	---|fE
        end

        --- Custom LSP hover for Neovim.

        --- Gets LSP style
        ---@param ... any
        ---@return hover.style__static
        local function get_style (...)
        	---|fS

******0 	local keys = vim.tbl_keys(hover.config);
******0 	local result = hover.config.default;

******0 	table.sort(keys);

******0 	for _, key in ipairs(keys) do
******0 		if key ~= "default" then
******0 			local val = hover.config[key];
******0 			local ran_cond, cond = pcall(val.condition, ...);

******0 			if ran_cond and cond then
        				result = vim.tbl_deep_extend("force", result, val);
******0 				break;
        			end
        		end
        	end

******0 	local final_val = {};

******0 	for k, v in pairs(result) do
******0 		if type(v) ~= "function" then
******0 			final_val[k] = v;
******0 		elseif k ~= "condition" then
******0 			local can_eval, value = pcall(v, ...);

******0 			if can_eval then
******0 				final_val[k] = value;
        			end
        		end
        	end

******0 	return final_val;

        	---|fE
        end

        ---@type integer, integer Hover buffer & window.

        --- Prepares the buffer for the hover window.

        	---|fS

******0 						vim.fn.winnr("#")

        ---@param quad "top_left" | "top_right" | "bottom_left" | "bottom_right" | "center"
        ---@param state boolean
        hover.update_quad = function (quad, state)
        	---|fS

******0 	if not _G.__used_quads then
******0 		_G.__used_quads = {
        			top_left = false,
        			top_right = false,

        			bottom_left = false,
******0 			bottom_right = false
******0 		};
        	end

******0 	if quad ~= "center" then
******0 		_G.__used_quads[quad] = state;
        	end

        	---|fE
        end

        ---@param window integer
        ---@param w integer
        ---@param h integer
        ---@return string | string[]
        ---@return "editor" | "cursor"
        ---@return "NE" | "NW" | "SE" | "SW"
        ---@return integer
        ---@return integer
        hover.__win_args = function (window, w, h)
        	---|fS

        	---@type [ integer, integer ]
******0 	local cursor = vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(window);
        	---@type table<string, integer>
******0 	local screenpos = vim.fn.screenpos(window, cursor[1], cursor[2]);

******0 	local screen_width = vim.o.columns - 2;
******0 	local screen_height = vim.o.lines - vim.o.cmdheight - 2;

******0 	local quad_pref = { "bottom_right", "bottom_left", "top_right", "top_left" };
******0 	local quads = {
        		---|fS

******0 		center = {
        			relative = "editor",
        			anchor = "NW",

        			row = math.ceil((vim.o.lines - h) / 2),
        			col = math.ceil((vim.o.columns - w) / 2),
******0 			border = "rounded"
        		},

******0 		top_left = {
        			condition = function ()
******0 				if h >= screenpos.row then
        					-- Not enough space above.
******0 					return false;
******0 				elseif screenpos.curscol <= w then
        					-- Not enough space before.
******0 					return false;
        				end

******0 				return true;
        			end,

        			relative = "cursor",
        			border = { "╭", "─", "╮", "│", "┤", "─", "╰", "│" },
        			anchor = "SE",
        			row = 0,
******0 			col = 1
        		},
******0 		top_right = {
        			condition = function ()
******0 				if h >= screenpos.row then
        					-- Not enough space above.
******0 					return false;
******0 				elseif screenpos.curscol + w > screen_width then
        					-- Not enough space after.
******0 					return false;
        				end

******0 				return true;
        			end,

        			relative = "cursor",
        			border = { "╭", "─", "╮", "│", "╯", "─", "├", "│" },
        			anchor = "SW",
        			row = 0,
******0 			col = 0
        		},

******0 		bottom_left = {
        			condition = function ()
******0 				if screenpos.row + h > screen_height then
        					-- Not enough space below.
******0 					return false;
******0 				elseif screenpos.curscol <= w then
        					-- Not enough space before.
******0 					return false;
        				end

******0 				return true;
        			end,

        			relative = "cursor",
        			border = { "╭", "─", "┤", "│", "╯", "─", "╰", "│" },
        			anchor = "NE",
        			row = 1,
******0 			col = 1
        		},
******0 		bottom_right = {
        			condition = function ()
******0 				if screenpos.row + h > screen_height then
        					-- Not enough space below.
******0 					return false;
******0 				elseif screenpos.curscol + w > screen_width then
        					-- Nor enough space after.
******0 					return false;
        				end

******0 				return true;
        			end,

        			relative = "cursor",
        			border = { "├", "─", "╮", "│", "╯", "─", "╰", "│" },
        			anchor = "NW",
        			row = 1,
******0 			col = 0
        		}

        		---|fE
******0 	};

******0 	for _, pref in ipairs(quad_pref) do
******0 		if _G.__used_quads and _G.__used_quads[pref] == true then
******0 			goto continue;
        		end

******0 		if not quads[pref] then
******0 			goto continue;
        		end

******0 		local quad = quads[pref];
******0 		local ran_cond, cond = pcall(quad.condition);

******0 		if ran_cond and cond then
******0 			hover.quad = pref;
******0 			return quad.border, quad.relative, quad.anchor, quad.row, quad.col;
        		end

        		::continue::
        	end

******0 	hover.quad = "center";
******0 	local fallback = quads.center;
******0 	return fallback.border, fallback.relative, fallback.anchor, fallback.row, fallback.col;

        	---|fE
        end

        --- Custom hover function
        ---@param window? integer
        hover.hover = function (window)
        	---|fS

        	window = window or vim.api.nvim_get_current_win();

******0 	if hover.window and vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(hover.window) then
******0 		vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(hover.window);
******0 		return;
        	end

        	---@type boolean, table
******0 	local got_position_params, position_params = pcall(vim.lsp.util.make_position_params, window, "utf-8");
        	---@type integer
******0 	local buf = vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(window);

******0 	if not got_position_params then
******0 		vim.api.nvim_echo({
******0 			{ "  Lsp hover ", "DiagnosticVirtualTextWarn" },
******0 			{ ": ", "@comment" },
******0 			{ "Couldn't get position parameters!", "Comment" }
******0 		}, false, {})
******0 		return;
        	end

******0 	vim.lsp.buf_request(buf, "textDocument/hover", position_params, function (err, result, ctx, config)
******0 		if err then
        			--- Error getting hover information.
******0 			vim.api.nvim_echo({
******0 				{ "  Lsp hover ", "DiagnosticVirtualTextError" },
******0 				{ ": ", "@comment" },
******0 				{ err.message, "Comment" }
******0 			}, true, {})
******0 			return;
******0 		elseif not result or not result.contents then
******0 			vim.api.nvim_echo({
******0 				{ "  Lsp.hover ", "DiagnosticVirtualTextError" },
******0 				{ ": ", "@comment" },
******0 				{ "No information available!", "Comment" }
******0 			}, false, {})
******0 			return;
        		end

******0 		hover.__prepare();

        		---@type integer
******0 		local client_id = ctx.client_id;
******0 		local client = vim.lsp.get_client_by_id(client_id) or {};
******0 		local client_name = client.name or "";

******0 		local _config = get_style(client_name, result, ctx);
******0 		local W, H = _config.width or math.floor(vim.o.columns * 0.25), _config.height or math.floor(vim.o.lines * 0.4);

******0 		_config.winopts = vim.tbl_extend("force", config or {}, _config.winopts or {});

******0 		local contents = result.contents or {};
        		---@type string[]
        		local lines

        		--[[
        			NOTE: LSP hover contents can be any of the followings,

        			1. Literal string.
        			2. A table(`{ kind = ..., value = ... }`).
        			3. A list(`{ kind = ..., value = ... }[]`).
        		]]
******0 		if type(contents) == "string" then
        			lines = vim.split(contents, "\n", { trimempty = true });
******0 			vim.bo[hover.buffer].ft = "markdown";
******0 		elseif vim.islist(contents) then
        			contents = contents[1];

        			lines = vim.split(contents.value or "", "\n", { trimempty = true });
******0 			vim.bo[hover.buffer].ft = contents.kind or "markdown";
        		else
        			lines = vim.split(contents.value or "", "\n", { trimempty = true });
******0 			vim.bo[hover.buffer].ft = contents.kind or "markdown";
        		end

        		-- Set content
******0 		vim.bo[hover.buffer].tw = W;
******0 		vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(hover.buffer, 0, -1, false, lines);

        		-- Initially open a hidden window.
        		-- We will calculate the wrapped text height.
        		-- Then we make it visible again.
******0 		if not hover.window or vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(hover.window) == false then
******0 			hover.window = vim.api.nvim_open_win(hover.buffer, false, {
        				relative = "cursor",

        				row = 1, col = 0,
        				width = W, height = H,

        				style = "minimal",
        				hide = true,
******0 			});
        		end

******0 		if hover.quad then
        			-- Reset any residual quadrant data.
******0 			hover.update_quad(hover.quad, false);
        		end

        		-- Set necessary options.
******0 		vim.wo[hover.window].wrap = true;
******0 		vim.wo[hover.window].linebreak = true;
******0 		vim.wo[hover.window].breakindent = true;

******0 		vim.wo[hover.window].conceallevel = 3;
******0 		vim.wo[hover.window].concealcursor = "ncv";

******0 		vim.wo[hover.window].foldmethod = "manual";

******0 		H = math.max(
        			1,
******0 			math.min(
******0 				vim.api.nvim_win_text_height(hover.window, { start_row = 0, end_row = -1 }).all,
        				H,
******0 				math.floor(vim.o.lines * 0.5)
        			)
******0 		);

        		-- Reset old text, otherwise it may break syntax highlighting.
******0 		vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(hover.buffer, 0, -1, false, lines);

******0 		local border, relative, anchor, row, col = hover.__win_args(window, W, H);

        		-- Make window visible.
******0 		vim.api.nvim_win_set_config(hover.window, vim.tbl_extend("force", {
        			relative = relative or "cursor",

        			row = row or 1, col = col or 0,
        			width = W, height = H,

        			anchor = anchor,

        			style = "minimal",

******0 		}, _config.winopts));

        		-- gets ignored.
******0 		vim.wo[hover.window].signcolumn = "no";

******0 		if type(_config.winhl) == "string" then
******0 			vim.wo[hover.window].winhl = _config.winhl;
        		end

******0 		vim.api.nvim_win_set_cursor(hover.window, { 1, 0 });
******0 		hover.update_quad(hover.quad, true);

******0 		if package.loaded["markview"] then

******0 				hybrid_mode = false
******0 			});
        		end
        	end);

        	---|fE
        end

        ---@param config? table<string, hover.style>
        hover.setup = function (config)
        	---|fS

******0 		hover.config = vim.tbl_extend("force", hover.config, config);

******0 	vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd("LspAttach", {

        --- Maps motions to callbacks.
        ---@class motions.map
        ---
        ---@field [string] fun(quqntifier: integer): nil

        ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        --[[
        Allows running `callbacks` when triggering `motions`.

        Usage,

        ```lua
        require("motions").add_event_listener({
        	gg = function ()
        		vim.print("hello world!");
        	end
        });
        ```
        ]]
      3 local motions = {}

        ---@type string Current mode shorthand.
      3 motions.mode = "n"

      6 vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd("ModeChanged", {
          callback = function(event)
******0     motions.mode = string.match(event.match, ":(.)$") or event.match
          end,
        })

        ---@type string Previous keys. Must be cleared after each motion!
      3 motions.previous = ""

        ---@type string Last successfully run motion.
      3 motions.last = ""

        --- Parses key presses.
        ---@param map motions.map
        ---@param _ string
        ---@param key string
        motions.parse = function(map, _, key)
          ---|fS

******0   map = map or {}

******0   local quantifier = tonumber(string.match(motions.previous, "%d+$")) or 0

          ---@param clear boolean
          ---@param motion string
          ---@param alt? string
          ---@param ... any
          local function exec_callback(clear, motion, alt, ...)
******0     if type(map) ~= "table" then
******0       return
******0     elseif clear then
******0       motions.previous = ""
            end

******0     motions.last = motion

******0     if alt and map[alt] then
******0       pcall(map[alt], ...)
            else
******0       pcall(map[motion], ...)
            end
          end

******0   local single_keys = {
            "h",
            "j",
            "k",
            "l",
            "0",
            "^",
            "$",
            "|",
            "G",
            ";",
            ",",
            "w",
            "W",
            "e",
            "E",
            "b",
            "B",
            "(",
            ")",
            "{",
            "}",
            "%",
            "H",
            "M",
            "L",

            "n",
            "N",
            "~",
          }

******0   if motions.mode ~= "n" then
            -- Wrong mode.
******0     motions.previous = ""
******0   elseif motions.previous == "" and string.match(key, "%d") then
            -- Quantifier.
******0     motions.previous = motions.previous .. key
******0   elseif string.match(motions.previous, "%d*[fFtT]") then
            -- Character finder.
******0     exec_callback(true, string.match(motions.previous, "[fFtT]$"), nil, quantifier, key)
******0   elseif vim.list_contains(single_keys, key) then
            -- Single letter motions.
******0     exec_callback(true, key, nil, quantifier)
******0   elseif string.match(motions.previous, "g['`]$") then
            -- g'{mark} or g`{mark}
******0     exec_callback(true, "g" .. key, nil, quantifier)
******0   elseif string.match(motions.previous, "g$") then
******0     local actions = {
              "_",
              "0",
              "^",
              "m",
              "M",
              "$",
              "k",
              "j",
              "g",
              "e",
              "E",
              ";",
              ",",
            }

******0     if vim.list_contains(actions, key) then
******0       exec_callback(true, "g" .. key, nil, quantifier)
******0     elseif key == "'" or key == "`" then
******0       motions.previous = motions.previous .. key
            else
******0       motions.previous = ""
            end
******0   elseif vim.list_contains({ "(", ")", "{", "}" }, key) then
            -- Sentence & paragraph movements.
******0     exec_callback(true, key, nil, quantifier)
******0   elseif string.match(motions.previous, "%]$") then
******0     local actions = {
              "]",
              "[",
              "'",
              "`",
              ")",
              "}",
              "m",
              "M",
              "#",
              "*",
              "/",
            }

******0     if vim.list_contains(actions, key) then
******0       exec_callback(true, "]" .. key, nil, quantifier)
            else
******0       motions.previous = ""
            end
******0   elseif string.match(motions.previous, "%[$") then
******0     local actions = {
              "[",
              "]",
              "'",
              "`",
              "(",
              "{",
              "m",
              "M",
              "#",
              "*",
              "/",
            }

******0     if vim.list_contains(actions, key) then
******0       exec_callback(true, "[" .. key, nil, quantifier)
            else
******0       motions.previous = ""
            end
******0   elseif string.match(motions.previous, "a$") then
******0     local actions = {
              "w",
              "W",
              "s",
              "p",
              "]",
              "[",
              "(",
              ")",
              "b",

              "t",

              "B",

******0     if vim.list_contains(actions, key) then
******0       exec_callback(true, "a" .. key, nil, quantifier)

******0       motions.previous = ""
            end
******0   elseif string.match(motions.previous, "i$") then
******0     local actions = {
              "w",
              "W",
              "s",
              "p",
              "]",
              "[",
              "(",
              ")",
              "b",
              ">",
              "<",
              "t",
              "{",
              "}",
              "B",
              '"',
              "'",
              "`",
            }

******0     if vim.list_contains(actions, key) then
******0       exec_callback(true, "i" .. key, nil, quantifier)
            else
******0       motions.previous = ""
            end
******0   elseif string.match(motions.previous, "['`]$") then
******0     local marker = string.match(motions.previous, "['`]$")
******0     local special = {
              "[",
              "]",
              "<",
              ">",
              "'",
              "`",
              '"',
              "^",
              ".",
              "(",
              ")",
              "{",
              "}",
            }

******0     if string.match(key, "[%a%d]") or vim.list_contains(special, key) then
              -- '{mark} or `{mark}
******0       exec_callback(true, marker .. key, nil, quantifier)
            else
******0       motions.previous = ""
            end
          else
******0     motions.previous = motions.previous .. key

******0     local keys = vim.tbl_keys(map)
******0     table.sort(keys)

******0     for _, _key in ipairs(keys) do
******0       if string.match(motions.previous, vim.pesc(_key) .. "$") then
******0         exec_callback(true, _key, nil, quantifier)
******0         motions.previous = ""
              end
            end
          end

          ---|fE
        end

        --- Shortens path segment length.
        ---@param path string
        ---@return string
        local function shorten_path(path)
          ---|fS

******0   local sep = string.sub(package.config, 1, 1)
******0   local as_raw = {
            "nvim$",
          }

          local function is_raw(str)
******0     for _, pattern in ipairs(as_raw) do
******0       if string.match(str, pattern) then
******0         return true
              end
            end

******0     return false
          end

******0   local parts = vim.split(path, sep, { trimempty = true })
******0   local shortened = {}

******0   for p, part in ipairs(parts) do
******0     if is_raw(part) or p == 1 or p == #parts then
******0       table.insert(shortened, part)
******0     elseif string.match(part, "^%.") then
******0       table.insert(shortened, vim.fn.strcharpart(part, 0, 2))
            else
******0       table.insert(shortened, vim.fn.strcharpart(part, 0, 1))
            end
          end

******0   return table.concat(shortened, sep)

          ---|fE
        end

        --[[
        Syncs terminal default `background` & `foreground` color with the current colorscheme.

        Usage,
        ```lua
        require("color_sync");
        ```
        ]]

      1 return {
          init = function()
            _G.terminal_color_sync = function()
              ---|fS

******0       local BG = vim.api.nvim_get_hl(0, { name = "Normal" }).bg or 1973806
******0       local FG = vim.api.nvim_get_hl(0, { name = "Normal" }).fg or 13489908

******0       if not BG or not FG then
******0         return
              end

******0       if _G.is_within_termux() then
******0         pcall(io.write, string.format("\x1b]11;#%06x\x1b\\", BG))
******0         pcall(io.write, string.format("\x1b]10;#%06x\x1b\\", FG))
              end

              ---|fE
            end

            --- Restores terminal's default colors.
            _G.terminal_color_restore = function()
              ---|fS

******0       if _G.is_within_termux() then
******0         pcall(io.write, "\x1b]110\x1b\\")
******0         pcall(io.write, "\x1b]111\x1b\\")
              end

              ---|fE
            end
          end,
          setup = function()
            --- Changes the background to match neovim's colorscheme
******0     vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd({ "UIEnter", "Colorscheme" }, {
              callback = function()
******0         _G.terminal_color_sync()
              end,
            })

            --- Reverts background color when leaving neovim
******0     vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd({ "UILeave" }, {
              callback = function()
******0         _G.terminal_color_restore()
              end,
            })

            --- Setup a user command to sync/restore it.
******0     vim.api.nvim_create_user_command("Color", function(cmd)
******0       local args = cmd.fargs

******0       if #args == 0 then
******0         _G.terminal_color_sync()
******0       elseif args[1] == "sync" then
******0         _G.terminal_color_sync()
******0       elseif args[1] == "restore" then
******0         _G.terminal_color_restore()
              end
******0     end, {
              desc = "Allows Syncing & Restoring terminal's color",
              nargs = "?",
              complete = function(arg_lead, cmdline)
******0         local args = vim.split(cmdline, " ", {})

******0         if #args > 2 or vim.list_contains({ "sync", "restore" }, args[2]) then
******0           return
                end

******0         local _c = {}

******0         for _, sub_command in ipairs({ "sync", "restore" }) do
******0           if string.match(sub_command, arg_lead or "") then
******0             table.insert(_c, sub_command)
                  end
                end

******0         return _c
              end,
            })
          end,
      1 }

        regen.regenerate_ts_config = function(user_config)
          --- New config.
******0   local config = {
            ["parser-directories"] = {},
            theme = {},
          }

          --- Current configuration.
******0   local current = get_config()
******0   local not_in_config = {}

******0   for _, path in ipairs(current["parser-directories"] or {}) do
******0     if vim.list_contains(config["parser-directories"] or {}, path) == false then
              -- Add non-existing paths.
******0       table.insert(not_in_config, path)
            end
          end

          -- Note: User paths have higher precedence.
******0   config["parser-directories"] = vim.list_extend(not_in_config, config["parser-directories"])

          -- User config takes highest precedence.
******0   config = vim.tbl_extend("force", config, user_config or {})

          ---@type string[] Tree-sitter highlight group names.
******0   local groups = vim.fn.getcompletion("@", "highlight")

******0   local def_value = vim.api.nvim_get_hl(0, { name = "Normal", link = false })

******0   for _, group in ipairs(groups) do
******0     local value = vim.api.nvim_get_hl(0, { name = group, link = false })

******0     if value.fg and value.fg == def_value.bg then
******0       value.fg = value.bg
            end

            ---@type string Tree-sitter theme group name.
******0     local ts_group = string.match(group, "^@(.+)$")

******0     config.theme[ts_group] = {
              -- Use hexadecimal values.
              color = value.fg and string.format("#%06x", value.fg) or nil,

              italic = value.italic,
              bold = value.bold,
              underline = value.underline,
            }
          end

******0   local has_dir = vim.uv.fs_stat(to_absolute("~/.config/tree-sitter/"))

******0   if not has_dir then
            -- Create the `~/.config/tree-eitter/` directory.
******0     vim.uv.fs_mkdir(to_absolute("~/.config/tree-sitter/"), 493)
          end

          ---@type string JSON
******0   local JSON = vim.json.encode(config)
******0   JSON = vim.fn.system("jq ", JSON)

******0   local file = io.open(to_absolute("~/.config/tree-sitter/config.json"), "w")

******0   if not file then
******0     return
          end

******0   file:write(JSON)
******0   file:close()
        end

        --- Theme system for bars.nvim statusline.
        --- Provides pre-defined style configurations with optional per-theme highlights.

      1 local M = {}

        --- Available themes.
      1 M.available = { "slashed", "minimal", "stealth", "bubble", "rainbow", "pure", "feline" }

        --- Get the statusline config for a given theme name.
        ---@param name string
        ---@return table? config
      1 function M.get(name)
      8   local sl_config = require("configs.bars.statusline")

      8   if name == "slashed" or name == "default" then
      1     return sl_config.default
          end

      7   if sl_config[name] then
      6     return sl_config[name]
          end

      1   return nil
        end

        --- Apply a theme at runtime by swapping the default component list.
        --- Resets base highlights and applies theme-specific highlights if defined.
        ---@param name string
      1 function M.apply(name)
******0   local config = M.get(name)

******0   if not config then
******0     vim.notify("Unknown statusline theme: " .. name, vim.log.levels.WARN)
******0     return
          end

          -- Reset base highlights to known-good state.
******0   local highlights = require("configs.bars.highlights")
******0   highlights.setup()

          -- Apply theme-specific highlights on top.
******0   if type(config.highlights) == "function" then
******0     config.highlights(highlights.colors)
          end

          -- Swap components.
******0   local statusline = require("bars.statusline")
******0   statusline.config.default.components = config.components

          -- Force re-render on all windows.
******0   for _, win in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_list_wins()) do
******0     pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_del_var, win, "_bars_statusline_style")
          end

******0   vim.cmd("redrawstatus!")
******0   vim.notify("Statusline theme: " .. name, vim.log.levels.INFO)
        end

            end

******0   end, {

            complete = function()
******0       return M.available
            end,

      1 return M

        local function parse_comment(line, prefix)
      5   local idx = line:find(prefix, 1, true)
      5   if not idx then
      1     return line, nil, nil
          end
      4   local code = line:sub(1, idx - 1)
      4   local rest = line:sub(idx + #prefix)
      8   if rest:sub(1, 1) == " " then
      8     rest = rest:sub(2)
          end

      4   local gs = rest:find("U%+%x+")
      4   if not gs then
      1     local base = vim.trim(rest)
      1     return code, base ~= "" and base or nil, nil
          end

      3   local candidate = rest:sub(gs)
      3   if not candidate:match("^U%+%x+%s+%S") then
      1     local base = vim.trim(rest)
      1     return code, base ~= "" and base or nil, nil
          end

      2   local s = gs - 1
      5   while s >= 1 do
      4     local c = rest:sub(s, s)
      4     if c == " " or c == "\t" or c == "|" then
      3       s = s - 1
            else

            end
          end
      4   local base = vim.trim(rest:sub(1, s))
      2   return code, base ~= "" and base or nil, candidate
        end

        --- Safely wraps the traditional require function that lua uses for importing modules
        --- Use in place of "require"
        --- @param path string
        ---     The path to import, this should be exactly what you would pass to require
        --- @function[opt] success_cb
        ---     Optional: If provided, this function will be called on a successfull import of
        ---     the provided "path". This function will be provided the imported module as
        ---     its single parameter.
        ---     Note: This is saved for calling later on reload
        ---     EG: success_cb(imported_module)
        --- @table[opt] import_opts
        ---     Optional: If provided, the following options are available to be used during
        ---     import.
        ---     hide_output = false,
        ---         -- When provided, this will hide anything that was printed, but still have it available
        ---         -- on the ImportStatus page
        ---     hide_errors = false,
        ---         -- When provided, this will hide anything that was printed to the errors, but still
        ---         -- be available on the ImportStatus page
        --- @return table, boolean
        ---
        --- Examples
        --- import("lualine", function(lualine) lualine.setup{ "do your module setup here" } end)
        --- import("netman") -- This module doesn't require setup so you dont need to specify it
        local import = function(path, success_cb, import_opts)
      5   if package.loaded[path] then
      1     if type(success_cb) == "function" then
******0       return success_cb(package.loaded[path])
            else
      1       return package.loaded[path], true
            end
          end

      4   import_opts = import_opts or {}
     16   for key, value in pairs(defaults) do
     12     if import_opts[key] == nil then
     12       import_opts[key] = value
            end
          end

      4   local logs = { print = {}, error = {}, replay = {} }
      4   local _print = print
      4   local _error = error
      4   local log_format = "%s - [%s] %s"

          -- print/error shim function to collect statements sent to both
          local print_shim = function(...)
******0     local print_safe_args = {}
******0     local _ = { ... }
******0     for i = 1, #_ do
******0       table.insert(print_safe_args, tostring(_[i]))
            end
            local print_log =
******0       string.format(log_format, path, os.date(log_fmt), table.concat(print_safe_args))
******0     table.insert(logs.print, print_log)
******0     if user_opts.import_enable_better_printing then
******0       table.insert(logs.replay, { level = "print", log = print_log })
            else
******0       table.insert(logs.replay, { level = "print", log = ... })
            end
          end
          -- redirect errors to print to avoid breaking import
          local error_shim = function(...)
******0     local print_safe_args = {}
******0     local _ = { ... }
******0     for i = 1, #_ do
******0       table.insert(print_safe_args, tostring(_[i]))
            end
            local error_log =
******0       string.format("ERROR: " .. log_format, path, os.date(log_fmt), table.concat(print_safe_args))
******0     table.insert(logs.error, error_log)
******0     if user_opts.import_enable_better_printing then
******0       table.insert(logs.replay, { level = "error", log = error_log })
            else
******0       table.insert(logs.replay, { level = "error", log = ... })
            end
          end
          -- global shenanigans
      4   _G.print = print_shim
      4   _G.error = error_shim

      4   local bm_opts = vim.tbl_deep_extend("force", import_opts.benchmarks, user_opts.benchmarks)

      4   local metrics = reporter.calc_metrics()
      4   prepare_benchmarks(path, bm_opts)
      4   local mod_status, mod_ret = pcall(require, path)
      4   reporter.calc_metrics(metrics)

          -- global shenanigans part 2, electric boogaloo
      4   _G.print = _print
      4   _G.error = _error
      4   if not import_opts.hide_output then
            -- Replay silenced print events from above print to neovim outside pcall.
            -- Prints dont always fire in with a require, but they _will_ during a pcall.
            -- Replicate behavior by replaying the events to neovim

      4     for _, log_info in ipairs(logs.replay) do

            end
          end

      4   state.status.info[path] = {
      4     import_message = mod_ret,
      4     import_start = metrics.start,
      4     import_end = metrics.finish,
      4     import_time = metrics.duration,
      4     load_n = metrics.load_n,
      4     print_logs = logs.print,
      4     error_logs = logs.error,
      4     success_cb = success_cb,
      4   }

      4   if not mod_status then
******0     state.status.failures[path] = 1
******0     state._error_state = true
          else
      4     if type(success_cb) == "function" then
******0       local cb_status, _ = pcall(success_cb, mod_ret)
******0       if not cb_status then
******0         error_shim(_)
******0         state.status.successes[path] = nil
******0         state.status.failures[path] = 1
              end
            else
      4       state.status.successes[path] = 1
      4       state.status.failures[path] = nil
            end
      4     finish_benchmarks(path)
          end

      4   if state._error_state and not state._error_printed then
******0     state._error_printed = true
******0     vim.notify("There was an error with your imports", 1)
          end

      4   return mod_ret, mod_status

lua/import/commands/reload.lua

      1 local import = require("import.commands.import")
      1 local state = require("import.commands.state")

        --- Will unload and reload the path (with import). Safe to call if path was not imported via the import function
        --- @string path
        ---     The path to import
        --- @function[opt] success_cb
        ---     Optional. Note, if not provided but one was used for the original import, we will simply call the
        ---     previous callback.
        ---     **To prevent this, pass "false" to success_cb, or provide a new callback**
        local reload = function(path, success_cb)
******0   package.loaded[path] = nil
******0   state.status.failures[path] = nil
******0   state.status.successes[path] = nil
******0   local cb_message = ""
******0   if state.status.info[path] then
******0     local _success_cb = state.status.info[path].success_cb
******0     if success_cb == nil then
******0       success_cb = _success_cb
******0       cb_message = "with cached callback"
            end
          end
******0   if cb_message == "" and success_cb then
******0     cb_message = "with provided callback"
          end
******0   local message = "Reloading"
******0   if not state.status.info[path] then
******0     message = "Importing"
          end
******0   state.status.info[path] = nil
******0   print(message, path, cb_message)
******0   import(path, success_cb)
        end

      1 return reload

        --- Returns import information about the provided path
        --- @param module string
        ---     The path to check import details for
        --- @return table
        ---     A table that contains either
        ---     {status = "unknown"}, if the path provided was not imported via the import method
        ---     or
        ---     {
        ---         status  = status,  -- String: This will be either success or failed
        ---         message = message, -- String: This will be either the error that was provided on import, or nil
        ---         errors  = errors,  -- Table: Any errors that the path threw during its import attempt
        ---         logs    = logs,    -- Table: Anything that was printed during the import (with approximate timestamps)
        ---     }

        local get_mod_status = function(module)
******0   local details = {
            status = "unknown",
            imported = false,
            import_time = -1,
            summary = "",
            report = "",
            message = {},
          }

******0   local mod_info = status.info[module]
******0   local mod_fail = status.failures[module]

******0   if not mod_info then
******0     details.summary = "Missing import state: " .. vim.inspect(module)
******0     return details
          end

******0   if mod_fail then
******0     details.status = "failed"
          else
******0     details.status = "success"
******0     details.imported = true
          end

******0   if type(mod_info.import_message) == "string" then
******0     details.stack = {}
******0     for line, _ in mod_info.import_message:gmatch("([^\r\n]*)") do
******0       table.insert(details.message, line)
******0       table.insert(details.stack, line)
            end
          end

******0   local keymap = {
******0     { "import_time", "import_time" },
******0     { "errors", "error_logs" },
******0     { "logs", "print_logs" },
******0     { "summary", "summary" },
******0     { "report", "report" },
******0     { "load_n", "load_n" },
          }

******0   for _, v in pairs(keymap) do
******0     details[v[1]] = mod_info[v[2]]
          end

          -- vim.dbglog(details)
******0   return details
        end

      1 return {
      1   status = status,
      1   get_mod_status = get_mod_status,
      1   get_failure_count = get_failure_count,
      1   get_success_count = get_success_count,
      1   get_all_modules = get_all_modules,
      1 }

        -- If collection can’t be split evenly, the final chunk will be the 
        -- remaining elements.
        -- @usage local t = _.chunk({'x', 'y', 'z', 1, 2, 3, 4, true , false}, 4)

                    break
                end
******0         i = i + 1
            end
******0     return t
        end

==============================================================================
lua/logger/debug/parsers/traceback.lua
==============================================================================
        local traceback = function()
          -- The main introspective function in the debug library is the debug.getinfo function. Its first parameter may be a function or a stack level. When you call debug.getinfo(foo) for some function foo, you get a table with some data about that function. The table may have the following fields:
          -- source --- Where the function was defined. If the function was defined in a string (through loadstring), source is that string. If the function was defined in a file, source is the file name prefixed with a `@´.
          -- short_src --- A short version of source (up to 60 characters), useful for error messages.
          -- linedefined --- The line of the source where the function was defined.
          -- what --- What this function is. Options are "Lua" if foo is a regular Lua function, "C" if it is a C function, or "main" if it is the main part of a Lua chunk.
          -- name --- A reasonable name for the function.
          -- namewhat --- What the previous field means. This field may be "global", "local", "method", "field", or "" (the empty string). The empty string means that Lua did not find a name for the function.
          -- nups --- Number of upvalues of that function.
          -- func --- The function itself; see later.
          -- When foo is a C function, Lua does not have much data about it. For such functions, only the fields what, name, and namewhat are relevant.
          -- When you call debug.getinfo(n) for some number n, you get data about the function active at that stack level. For instance, if n is 1, you get data about the function doing the call. (When n is 0, you get data about getinfo itself, a C function.) If n is larger than the number of active functions in the stack, debug.getinfo returns nil. When you query an active function, calling debug.getinfo with a number, the result table has an extra field, currentline, with the line where the function is at that moment. Moreover, func has the function that is active at that level.
          -- The field name is tricky. Remember that, because functions are first-class values in Lua, a function may not have a name, or may have several names. Lua tries to find a name for a function by looking for a global variable with that value, or else looking into the code that called the function, to see how it was called. This second option works only when we call getinfo with a number, that is, we get information about a particular invocation.
          -- The getinfo function is not efficient. Lua keeps debug information in a form that does not impair program execution; efficient retrieval is a secondary goal here. To achieve better performance, getinfo has an optional second parameter that selects what information to get. With this parameter, it does not waste time collecting data that the user does not need. The format of this parameter is a string, where each letter selects a group of data, according to the following table:
          -- `n´	selects fields name and namewhat
          -- `f´	selects field func
          -- `S´	selects fields source, short_src, what, and linedefined
          -- `l´	selects field currentline
          -- `u´	selects field nup
      1   local level = 1
      1   local out = {}
          while true do
     16     local info = debug.getinfo(level, "Sln")
     16     if not info then
      1       break
            end
     15     local msg = ""
     15     if info.what == "C" then -- is a C function?
      3       msg = level .. ": " .. "C function"
      3       if type(info.name) == "string" and info.name ~= "" then
      3         msg = msg .. " -> " .. info.namewhat .. ":" .. info.name
              end
            else -- a Lua function
              -- out = out .. '\n' .. string.format('[%s]:%d', info.short_src, info.currentline)
     12       msg = string.format("%d:[%s]:%d", level, info.source, info.currentline)
     12       if type(info.name) == "string" and info.name ~= "" then
      8         msg = msg .. " -> " .. info.namewhat .. ":" .. info.name
              end
            end
     15     table.insert(out, msg)
     15     level = level + 1
          end
      1   return out
        end

      1 return traceback

lua/logger/structlog/formatters/init.lua
==============================================================================
        --- Entry point for loading formatters.
        -- StructlLog is completely flexible about how the resulting log entry is emitted.
        -- Since each log entry is a dictionary, its message can be formatted to any format.
        --
        -- Internally, formatters are processors whose return value (a log entry with formatted message)
        -- is passed into sinks that are responsible for the output of your message.
        -- StructLog comes with multiple useful formatters out-of-the-box.
        -- @module structlog.formatters

        --- Formatter Module
        -- @table M
        -- @field Format Format log entries using a string format.
        -- @field FormatColorizer Format and Colorize log entries.
        -- Note that it is only available for the Console sink.
