lua/logger/structlog/processors/init.lua
==============================================================================
        --- Entry point for loading processors.
        -- A log processor is a regular callable, i.e a function or
        -- an instance of a table with a __call() method.
        --
        -- Each log entry goes through a processor pipeline that is just a chain of functions
        -- that receive a dictionary and return a new dictionary that gets fed into the next function.
        -- That allows for simple but powerful data manipulation.
        -- @module structlog.processors

        --- Processor Module
        -- @table M
        -- @field Timestamper Add a 'timestamp' entry.
        -- @field StackWriter Add entry in ['file', 'line'].
        -- @field Namer Add a 'logger_name' entry.
      2 local M = {
      2   Timestamper = require("logger.structlog.processors.timestamper"),
      2   StackWriter = require("logger.structlog.processors.stack_writer"),
      2   Namer = require("logger.structlog.processors.namer"),
        }

      2 return M

        -- - **[1]**(`string`) lhs **(required)**
        -- - **[2]**(`string|fun()`) rhs **(optional)**when present, it will create the mapping
        -- - **desc**(`string|fun():string`) description **(required for non-groups)**
        -- - **group**(`string|fun():string`) group name **(optional)**
        -- - **mode**(`string|string[]`) mode **(optional, defaults to "n")**
        -- - **cond**(`boolean|fun():boolean`) condition to enable the mapping **(optional)**
        -- - **hidden**(`boolean`) hide the mapping **(optional)**
        -- - **icon**(`string|wk.Icon|fun():(wk.Icon|string)`) icon spec **(optional)**
        -- - **proxy**(`string`) proxy to another mapping **(optional)**
        -- - **expand**(`fun():wk.Spec`) nested mappings **(optional)**
        -- - any other option valid for `vim.keymap.set`. These are only used for creating mappings.
        -- When `desc`, `group`, or `icon` are functions, they are evaluated every time the popup is shown.
        -- wk.add({

      1 local defaults = {
          -- Manual mode doesn't automatically change your root directory, so you have

          -- order matters: if one is not detected, the other is used as fallback. You

      1   detection_methods = { "lsp", "pattern" },

      2 function M.lsp_workspace_root(client, fname)
******0   local root_dir = false
******0   local workspace_folders = client.workspace_folders or client.workspaceFolders
******0   local is_windows = vim.uv.os_uname().version:match("Windows")
******0   fname = vim.fn.fnamemodify(vim.fn.resolve(fname), ":p")
******0   local sep = is_windows and "\\" or "/"
******0   local fname_parts = vim.split(fname, sep, { trimempty = true })
******0   if workspace_folders then
******0     for _, schema in pairs(workspace_folders) do
******0       local matched = true
******0       local root = vim.uv.fs_realpath(schema.name)
******0       local root_parts = vim.split(root, sep, { trimempty = true })

******0       for i = 1, #root_parts do
******0         if root_parts[i] ~= fname_parts[i] then
******0           matched = false
                  break
                end
              end

******0       if matched then
******0         root_dir = schema.name
                break
              end
            end
          end
******0   return root_dir
        end

      2 function M.find_lsp_root()

          -- Returns nil or string
******0   local options = require("project_nvim.config").get()

******0   local original_bufnr = vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
******0   local buf_ft = vim.api.nvim_get_option_value("filetype", { buf = original_bufnr })
******0   local clients = vim.lsp.get_clients({ bufnr = original_bufnr })

******0   local fname = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_name(original_bufnr)
          -- local fname = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_name(0)
          local root_dir = nil

******0   if next(clients) == nil then
******0     return nil
          end

******0   for _, client in pairs(clients) do
******0     if not vim.tbl_contains(options.ignore_lsp, client.name) then

******0       local workspace_folders = client.workspace_folders
******0       fname = uv.fs_realpath(fname) or vim.fn.fnamemodify(vim.fn.resolve(fname), ":p")
******0       if workspace_folders then
******0         for _, schema in ipairs(workspace_folders) do
******0           local ws_dir = uv.fs_realpath(schema.name)
******0           if ws_dir and fname:sub(1, ws_dir:len()) == ws_dir then
******0             root_dir = schema.name
******0             return root_dir, client.name

                end

******0       if filetypes and vim.tbl_contains(filetypes, buf_ft) then
******0         if not vim.tbl_contains(options.ignore_lsp, client.name) then
******0           root_dir = M.lsp_workspace_root(client, fname) or client.root_dir
******0           return root_dir, client.name

              end

      2 function M.find_pattern_root()
******0   local options = require("project_nvim.config").get()
******0   local start_dir = vim.fn.expand("%:p:h", true)

          -- Cache for directory listings (persists across all searches)
******0   local dir_cache = {}
          -- Cache for compiled glob patterns
******0   local glob_cache = {}

          local function get_parent(path)
******0     local parent = path:match("^(.*)/")
******0     return (parent == "" or not parent) and "/" or parent
          end

          local function get_dir_name(path)
******0     return path:match("([^/]+)$")
          end

          local function get_files(dir)
******0     local cached = dir_cache[dir]
******0     if cached then
******0       return cached
            end

******0     local files = {}
******0     local handle = uv.fs_scandir(dir)
******0     if handle then
******0       local name = uv.fs_scandir_next(handle)
******0       while name do
******0         files[#files + 1] = name
******0         name = uv.fs_scandir_next(handle)
              end
            end
******0     dir_cache[dir] = files
******0     return files
          end

          local function get_glob_pattern(identifier)
******0     local cached = glob_cache[identifier]
******0     if cached then
******0       return cached
            end
******0     local pattern = glob.globtopattern(identifier)
******0     glob_cache[identifier] = pattern
******0     return pattern
          end

          -- Match functions for different pattern types
          local function is_dir_name(dir, identifier)
******0     return get_dir_name(dir) == identifier
          end

          local function is_subdir_of(dir, identifier)
******0     local path = get_parent(dir)
******0     while path and path ~= "/" do
******0       if get_dir_name(path) == identifier then
******0         return true
              end
******0       local parent = get_parent(path)
******0       if parent == path then
                break
              end
******0       path = parent
            end
******0     return get_dir_name("/") == identifier
          end

          local function is_child_of(dir, identifier)
******0     return get_dir_name(get_parent(dir)) == identifier
          end

          local function has_file(dir, identifier)
******0     local files = get_files(dir)
******0     local pattern = get_glob_pattern(identifier)
******0     for i = 1, #files do
******0       if files[i]:match(pattern) then
******0         return true
              end
            end
******0     return false
          end

          local function match_pattern(dir, pattern)
******0     local first_char = pattern:sub(1, 1)
******0     if first_char == "=" then
******0       return is_dir_name(dir, pattern:sub(2))
******0     elseif first_char == "^" then
******0       return is_subdir_of(dir, pattern:sub(2))
******0     elseif first_char == ">" then
******0       return is_child_of(dir, pattern:sub(2))
            else
******0       return has_file(dir, pattern)
            end
          end

          -- Search all parent directories for any pattern in the given list
          -- Patterns in a group are checked together at each directory level
          local function search_parents_for_group(patterns)
******0     local dir = start_dir

******0     while dir do
******0       for i = 1, #patterns do
******0         local pattern = patterns[i]

                -- Skip nil or non-string patterns
******0         if type(pattern) ~= "string" then
                  goto continue
                end

******0         local exclude = false
******0         if pattern:sub(1, 1) == "!" then
******0           exclude = true
******0           pattern = pattern:sub(2)
                end

******0         if match_pattern(dir, pattern) then
******0           if exclude then
                    -- Exclusion matched: skip this directory, continue to parent
                    break
                  else
******0             return dir, patterns[i]
                  end
                end

                ::continue::
              end

******0       local parent = get_parent(dir)
******0       if parent == dir then
                break
              end
******0       dir = parent
            end

******0     return nil, nil
          end

          -- Process patterns/groups in order
          -- Groups (tables) search all parents for any pattern in the group before moving on
          -- Single patterns (strings) are treated as a group of one
******0   for _, entry in ipairs(options.patterns) do
******0     local patterns = type(entry) == "table" and entry or { entry }
******0     local result_dir, matched_pattern = search_parents_for_group(patterns)
******0     if result_dir then
******0       return result_dir, "pattern " .. matched_pattern
            end
          end

******0   return nil
        end

      2 function M.get_project_root()
          -- returns project root, as well as method

      3   local options = require("project_nvim.config").get()
      3   if type(options) ~= "table" or type(options.detection_methods) ~= "table" then

lua/project_nvim/utils/globtopattern.lua

        -- Credits for this module goes to: David Manura
        -- https://github.com/davidm/lua-glob-pattern

      2 local M = { _TYPE = "module", _NAME = "globtopattern", _VERSION = "0.2.1.20120406" }

      2 function M.globtopattern(g)
          -- Some useful references:
          -- - apr_fnmatch in Apache APR.  For example,
          --   http://apr.apache.org/docs/apr/1.3/group__apr__fnmatch.html
          --   which cites POSIX 1003.2-1992, section B.6.

******0   local p = "^" -- pattern being built
******0   local i = 0 -- index in g
          local c -- char at index i in g.

          -- unescape glob char
          local function unescape()
******0     if c == "\\" then
******0       i = i + 1
******0       c = g:sub(i, i)
******0       if c == "" then
******0         p = "[^]"
******0         return false
              end
            end
******0     return true
          end

          -- escape pattern char
          local function escape(c)
******0     return c:match("^%w$") and c or "%" .. c
          end

          -- Convert tokens at end of charset.
          local function charset_end()
******0     while 1 do
******0       if c == "" then
******0         p = "[^]"
******0         return false
******0       elseif c == "]" then
******0         p = p .. "]"
                break
              else
******0         if not unescape() then
                  break
                end
******0         local c1 = c
******0         i = i + 1
******0         c = g:sub(i, i)
******0         if c == "" then
******0           p = "[^]"
******0           return false
******0         elseif c == "-" then
******0           i = i + 1
******0           c = g:sub(i, i)
******0           if c == "" then
******0             p = "[^]"
******0             return false
******0           elseif c == "]" then
******0             p = p .. escape(c1) .. "%-]"
                    break
                  else
******0             if not unescape() then
                      break
                    end
******0             p = p .. escape(c1) .. "-" .. escape(c)
                  end
******0         elseif c == "]" then
******0           p = p .. escape(c1) .. "]"
                  break
                else
******0           p = p .. escape(c1)
******0           i = i - 1 -- put back
                end
              end
******0       i = i + 1
******0       c = g:sub(i, i)
            end
******0     return true
          end

          -- Convert tokens in charset.
          local function charset()
******0     i = i + 1
******0     c = g:sub(i, i)
******0     if c == "" or c == "]" then
******0       p = "[^]"
******0       return false
******0     elseif c == "^" or c == "!" then
******0       i = i + 1
******0       c = g:sub(i, i)
******0       if c == "]" then
              -- ignored
              else
******0         p = p .. "[^"
******0         if not charset_end() then
******0           return false
                end
              end
            else
******0       p = p .. "["
******0       if not charset_end() then
******0         return false
              end
            end
******0     return true
          end

          -- Convert tokens.
******0   while 1 do
******0     i = i + 1
******0     c = g:sub(i, i)
******0     if c == "" then
******0       p = p .. "$"
              break
******0     elseif c == "?" then
******0       p = p .. "."
******0     elseif c == "*" then
******0       p = p .. ".*"
******0     elseif c == "[" then
******0       if not charset() then
                break
              end
******0     elseif c == "\\" then
******0       i = i + 1
******0       c = g:sub(i, i)
******0       if c == "" then
******0         p = p .. "\\$"
                break
              end
******0       p = p .. escape(c)
            else
******0       p = p .. escape(c)
            end
          end
******0   return p
        end

      2 return M

==============================================================================
lua/registers/init.lua
==============================================================================
        ---@brief [[
        ---Registers.nvim is a minimal but very configurable Neovim plugin.
        ---Keys can be bound using functions, to make it easier for use I've made all functions except `registers.setup()` return callback functions that can be configured and passed to fields in the `bind_keys` example.
        ---
        ---For example, to apply a delay of a second after selecting the register with its key (for example pressing the '0' key to apply the '0' register when it's open):
        ---use {
        ---  "tversteeg/registers.nvim",
        ---  config = function()
        ---    local registers = require("registers")
        ---    registers.setup({
        ---      bind_keys = {
        ---        registers = registers.apply_register({ delay = 1 }),
        ---      },
        ---    })
        ---  end
        ---}
        ---<
        ---@brief ]]

      3 local ui = require("registers.ui")
      3 local mappings = require("registers.mappings")
      3 local state = require("registers.state")
      3 local config = require("registers.config")
      3 local log = require("logger").init("registers")

        ---@class registers
        ---@field options options
      3 local registers = {}

        ---@mod setup `registers.setup` configuration options.

        ---`require("registers").setup({...})`
        ---@class options
        ---@field show string Which registers to show and in what order. Default is `"*+\"-/_=#%.0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz:"`.
        ---@field show_empty boolean Show the registers which aren't filled in a separate line. Default is `true`.
        ---@field register_user_command boolean Whether to register the `:Registers` user command. Default is `true`.
        ---@field system_clipboard boolean Transfer selected register to the system clipboard. Default is `true`.
        ---@field trim_whitespace boolean Don't show whitespace at the begin and and of the registers, won't change the output from applying the register. Default is `true`.
        ---@field hide_only_whitespace boolean Treat registers with only whitespace as empty registers. Default is `true`.
        ---@field show_register_types boolean Show how the register will be applied in the sign bar, the characters can be customized in the `symbols` table. Default is `true`.
        ---@field bind_keys bind_keys_options|boolean Which keys to bind, `true` maps all keys and `false` maps no keys.
        ---@field events events_options Functions that will be called when certain events happen.
        ---@field symbols symbols_options Symbols used to replace text in the previous buffer.
        ---@field window window_options Floating window
        ---@field sign_highlights sign_highlights_options Highlights for the sign section of the window

        ---@alias register_mode
        ---| '"insert"' # Insert the register's contents like when in insert mode and pressing <C-R>.
        ---| '"paste"' # Insert the register's contents by pretending a pasting action, similar to pressing "*reg*p, cannot be used in insert mode.
        ---| '"motion"' # Create a motion from the register, similar to pressing "*reg* (without pasting it yet).

        ---`require("registers").setup({ bind_keys = {...} })`
        ---@class bind_keys_options
        ---@field normal fun()|false Function to map to " in normal mode to display the registers window, `false` to disable the binding. Default is `registers.show_window({ mode = "motion" })`.
        ---@field visual fun()|false Function to map to " in visual mode to display the registers window, `false` to disable the binding. Default is `registers.show_window({ mode = "motion" })`.
        ---@field insert fun()|false Function to map to <C-R> in insert mode to display the registers window, `false` to disable the binding. Default is `registers.show_window({ mode = "insert" })`.
        ---@field registers fun(register:string,mode:register_mode) Function to map to the register selected by pressing it's key. Default is `registers.apply_register()`.
        ---@field [string] fun()|false Function to map to the custom key binding in the registers window.
        ---@field return_key fun(register:string,mode:register_mode) Deprecated, function to map to <CR> in the registers window. Default is `registers.apply_register()`.
        ---@field escape fun(register:string,mode:register_mode) Deprecated, function to map to <ESC> in the registers window. Default is `registers.close_window()`.
        ---@field ctrl_n fun()|false Deprecated, function to map <C-N> in the registers window. Default is `registers.move_cursor_down()`.
        ---@field ctrl_p fun()|false Deprecated, function to map <C-P> in the registers window. Default is `registers.move_cursor_up()`.
        ---@field ctrl_j fun()|false Deprecated, function to map <C-J> in the registers window. Default is `registers.move_cursor_down()`.
        ---@field ctrl_k fun()|false Deprecated, function to map <C-K> in the registers window. Default is `registers.move_cursor_up()`.
        ---@field delete fun()|false Deprecated, function to map <DEL> in the registers window. Default is `registers.clear_highlighted_register()`.
        ---@field backspace fun()|false Deprecated, function to map <BS> in the registers window. Default is `registers.clear_highlighted_register()`.

        ---`require("registers").setup({ events = {...} })`
        ---@class events_options
        ---@field on_register_highlighted fun()|false Function that's called when a new register is highlighted when the window is open. Default is `registers.preview_highlighted_register({ if_mode = { "insert", "paste" } })`.

        ---@alias window_border
        ---| '"none"'
        ---| '"single"'
        ---| '"double"'
        ---| '"rounded"'
        ---| '"solid"'
        ---| '"shadow"'
        ---| 'string[]' # An array of eight strings which each corner and side character.

        ---`require("registers").setup({ window = {...} })`
        ---@class window_options
        ---@field max_width? number Maximum width of the window, normal size will be calculated based on the size of the longest register. Default is `100`.
        ---@field highlight_cursorline? boolean Whether to create key mappings for the register values inside the window. Default is `true`.
        ---@field border? window_border Border style of the window. Default is `"none"`.
        ---@field transparency? integer Transparency of the window, value can be between 0-100, 0 disables it. Default is `10`.

        ---`require("registers").setup({ symbols = {...} })`
        ---@class symbols_options
        ---@field newline? string Symbol to show for a line break character, can not be the `"\\n"` symbol, use `"\\\\n"` (two backslashes) instead. Default is `"⏎"`.
        ---@field space? string Symbol to show for a space character. Default is `" "`.
        ---@field tab? string Symbol to show for a tab character. Default is `"·"`.
        ---@field register_type_charwise? string Symbol to show next to the sign to signify that the register will be applied in a character by character way. Default is `"ᶜ"`.
        ---@field register_type_linewise? string Symbol to show next to the sign to signify that the register will be applied in a line by line way. Default is `"ˡ"`.
        ---@field register_type_blockwise? string Symbol to show next to the sign to signify that the register will be applied as a horizontal block, ignoring line endings. Default is `"ᵇ"`.

        ---`require("registers").setup({ sign_highlights = {...} })`
        ---@class sign_highlights_options
        ---@field cursorline? string Highlight group for when the cursor is over the line. Default is `"Visual"`.
        ---@field selection? string Highlight group for the selection registers, `*+`. Default is `"Constant"`.
        ---@field default? string Highlight group for the default register, `"`. Default is `"Function"`.
        ---@field unnamed? string Highlight group for the unnamed register, `\\`. Default is `"Statement"`.
        ---@field read_only? string Highlight group for the read only registers, `:.%`. Default is `"Type"`.
        ---@field alternate_buffer? string Highlight group for the alternate buffer register, `#`. Default is `"Type"`.
        ---@field expression? string Highlight group for the expression register, `=`. Default is `"Exception"`.
        ---@field black_hole? string Highlight group for the black hole register, `_`. Default is `"Error"`.
        ---@field last_search? string Highlight group for the last search register, `/`. Default is `"Operator"`.
        ---@field delete? string Highlight group for the delete register, `-`. Default is `"Special"`.
        ---@field yank? string Highlight group for the yank register, `0`. Default is `"Delimiter"`.
        ---@field history? string Highlight group for the history registers, `1-9`. Default is `"Number"`.
        ---@field named? string Highlight group for the named registers, `a-z`. Default is `"Todo"`.

        ---Let the user configure this plugin.
        ---
        ---This will also register the default user commands and key bindings.
        ---@param options? options Plugin configuration options.
        ---@usage `require("registers").setup({})`
      3 function registers.setup(options)
      3   local opts = config.setup(options)

          -- Create the user command to manually open the window with :Registers
      3   if opts.register_user_command then
      6     vim.api.nvim_create_user_command("Registers", ui.show_window({ mode = "paste" }), {})
          end

          -- Create a namespace for the highlights and signs
      3   state._namespace = vim.api.nvim_create_namespace("registers")
      3   mappings.setup()

      3   log:info("registers.nvim setup complete")
        end

      3 return registers

        ---@private
        ---Whether a key should be bound.
        ---@param option string Which item from bind_keys should be checked
        ---@return boolean Whether the key should be bound
        ---@nodiscard
        local _key_should_be_bound = function(option)
      9   local options = config.get()
      9   if type(options.bind_keys) == "boolean" then
******0     return options.bind_keys --[[@as boolean]]
          else
      9     return options.bind_keys[option]
          end
        end

        ---@private
        ---Set the key bindings for the window.
        local _set_bindings = function()
******0   local options = config.get()

          -- Helper function for setting the keymap for all buffer modes
          local set_keymap_all_modes = function(key, callback)
******0     if callback == nil then
******0       log:warn(string.format("no callback for key '%s', skipping", key))
******0       return
            end

******0     local map_options = {
              nowait = true,
              buffer = state._buffer,
            }

******0     map("n", key, callback, map_options)
******0     map("i", key, callback, map_options)
******0     map("x", key, callback, map_options)
          end

          -- Resolve a bind_keys callback by trying multiple key name variants.
          local resolve_binding = function(...)
******0     if type(options.bind_keys) ~= "table" then
******0       return nil
            end
******0     for _, name in ipairs({ ... }) do
******0       if options.bind_keys[name] ~= nil then
******0         return options.bind_keys[name]
              end
            end
******0     return nil
          end

          -- Map all register keys
******0   if _key_should_be_bound("registers") then
******0     for key, callback in pairs(state._mappings) do
******0       set_keymap_all_modes(key, callback)
            end
          end

          -- Map the keys for moving up and down. The user config may use either
          -- snake_case (ctrl_k) or angle-bracket notation (<C-k>, k, j), so try both.
******0   if _key_should_be_bound("k") then
******0     set_keymap_all_modes("k", resolve_binding("k", "ctrl_k", "<C-k>"))
          end
******0   if _key_should_be_bound("j") then
******0     set_keymap_all_modes("j", resolve_binding("j", "ctrl_j", "<C-j>"))
          end
******0   if _key_should_be_bound("ctrl_p") then
******0     set_keymap_all_modes("<c-p>", resolve_binding("ctrl_p", "<C-p>"))
          end
******0   if _key_should_be_bound("ctrl_n") then
******0     set_keymap_all_modes("<c-n>", resolve_binding("ctrl_n", "<C-n>"))
          end

******0   log:debug(string.format("set window bindings on buffer %s", tostring(state._buffer)))
        end

        ---@private
        ---Create a map for global key binding with a callback function.
        ---@param index string Key of the function in the `bind_keys` table.
        ---@param key string Which key to press.
        ---@param mode register_mode Which mode to register the key.
        local _bind_global_key = function(index, key, mode)
      9   local options = config.get()

     18   if _key_should_be_bound(index) then
     18     map(mode, key, function()
              -- Don't open the registers window in a telescope prompt or in a non-modifiable buffer
              if
******0         not vim.bo.modifiable
******0         or vim.bo.filetype == "TelescopePrompt"
******0         or vim.bo.filetype == "DressingInput"
              then
******0         return vim.api.nvim_replace_termcodes(key, true, true, true)
              else
                -- Call the callback function passed to the options
******0         return options.bind_keys[index]()
              end
      9     end, { expr = true })
      9     log:debug(string.format("bound global key '%s' in mode '%s'", key, mode))
          end
        end

      3   local options = config.get()

      3   if not options.bind_keys then

          end

          -- Add the other user-defined keys to the map
      3   local reserved_keys = {

            k = true,

            insert = true,

    138   for _, register in ipairs(_all_registers) do
            -- Pressing the character of a register will also apply it
    135     state._mappings[register] = function()

******0       ui._move_cursor_to_register(register)

******0       options.bind_keys.registers(register, state._mode)

            -- Also map uppercase registers if applicable
    270     if register:upper() ~= register then

******0         ui._move_cursor_to_register(register)

******0         options.bind_keys.registers(register:upper(), state._mode)

      6   log:debug(string.format("built mappings for %d keys", vim.tbl_count(state._mappings)))

     19 local state = require("registers.state")
     19 local config = require("registers.config")

        local highlight_lines = function(lines)
******0   local options = config.get()
******0   local hl_group = vim.tbl_get(options, "sign_highlights", "register_preview") or "Normal"
******0   local hl_lines = {}
******0   for _, line in ipairs(lines) do
******0     local line_parts = { line, hl_group }
******0     table.insert(hl_lines, { line_parts })
          end
          -- *vim.lsp.util.convert_input_to_markdown_lines()*
******0   return hl_lines
        end

        ---Show a preview of the highlighted register in the target buffer.
        ---Currently this overlays the text, waiting for https://github.com/neovim/neovim/pull/9496 to merge.
        ---@param options callback_options Options for firing the callback.
        ---@return function callback Function that can be used to pass to configuration options with callbacks.
        local preview_register = function(options)
     22   local utils = require("registers.utils")
     22   return utils._handle_callback_options(options --[[@as callback_options]], function()
            -- Get the register contents for the current line as a table
******0     local register_info = utils._register_info()

            -- Do nothing when an invalid line is selected
******0     if type(register_info) ~= "table" then
******0       return
            end

******0     log:debug("preview register: " .. tostring(register_info.register))

******0     local register_lines = register_info.regcontents

            -- Add the highlight to the lines
******0     local lines = highlight_lines(register_lines)

            -- Clear the previous extmarks
******0     vim.api.nvim_buf_clear_namespace(state._preview_buffer, state._namespace, 0, -1)

            -- Get the cursor position of the main buffer
******0     local line, col = unpack(vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(state._preview_window))

******0     vim.api.nvim_buf_set_extmark(
******0       state._preview_buffer,
******0       state._namespace,
******0       line - 1,
              col,
******0       { virt_lines = lines }
              -- { virt_text = lines, virt_text_win_col = col }
            )
            -- if #lines == 1 then
            --   -- Display the register content
            --   vim.api.nvim_buf_set_extmark(
            --     state._preview_buffer,
            --     state._namespace,
            --     line - 1,
            --     col,
            --     { virt_text = lines, virt_text_win_col = col }
            --   )
            -- else
            --   -- Display the register content
            -- end
          end)
        end
        -- • virt_lines : virtual lines to add next to this mark This
        --   should be an array over lines, where each line in turn is
        --   an array over [text, highlight] tuples. In general, buffer
        --   and window options do not affect the display of the text.
        --   In particular 'wrap' and 'linebreak' options do not take
        --   effect, so the number of extra screen lines will always
        --   match the size of the array. However the 'tabstop' buffer
        --   option is still used for hard tabs. By default lines are
        --   placed below the buffer line containing the mark.
        -- • virt_lines_above: place virtual lines above instead.
        -- • virt_lines_leftcol: Place extmarks in the leftmost column
        --   of the window, bypassing sign and number columns.

     19 return { preview_register = preview_register }

     19 local uv = vim.uv

        ---@private
        ---Close the window.
        local _close_window = function()
          -- Close the window, this should also close the buffer
******0   if state._window then
******0     pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_close, state._window, true)
******0     state._window = nil
          end

          -- But if the buffer is created and interrupted before the window is opened this won't happen
******0   if state._buffer then
******0     pcall(vim.api.nvim_buf_delete, state._buffer, { force = true })
******0     state._buffer = nil
          end

          -- Clear the namespace if it's on the preview
******0   if state._preview_buffer and state._namespace then
******0     pcall(vim.api.nvim_buf_clear_namespace, state._preview_buffer, state._namespace, 0, -1)
          end

******0   log:debug("window closed")
        end

        ---@private
        ---Handle the CursorMoved autocmd.
        local _cursor_moved = function()
******0   local cursor = unpack(vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(state._window))
******0   local options = config.get()

          -- Skip horizontal movement
******0   if state._previous_cursor_line == cursor then
******0     return
          end
******0   state._previous_cursor_line = cursor

          -- Trigger the highlight change event
******0   options.events.on_register_highlighted()
        end

        ---@private
        ---Fill the window's buffer.
        local _fill_window = function()
******0   local options = config.get()

          -- Don't allow the buffer to be modified
******0   vim.api.nvim_set_option_value("modifiable", true, { buf = state._buffer })

          -- Create an array of lines for all the registers
******0   local lines = {}
******0   for i = 1, #state._register_values do
******0     local register = state._register_values[i]

******0     lines[i] = register.line
          end

          -- Add the empty line
******0   if options.show_empty then
******0     lines[#lines + 1] = "Empty: " .. table.concat(state._empty_registers, " ")
          end

          -- Write the lines to the buffer
******0   vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(state._buffer, 0, -1, false, lines)

          -- Create signs and highlights for the register itself
******0   for i = 1, #state._register_values do
******0     local register = state._register_values[i]

******0     local sign_text = register.register
            -- Add the register type symbol if applicable
******0     if options.show_register_types then
******0       sign_text = sign_text .. register.type_symbol
            end

            -- Create signs for the register itself, and highlight the line
******0     vim.api.nvim_buf_set_extmark(state._buffer, state._namespace, i - 1, 0, {
              id = i,

        ---@private

        local _apply_register = function(register, keep_open_until_keypress)
******0   local ui = require("registers.ui")

******0   register = _register_symbol(register)

******0   elseif keep_open_until_keypress and state._mode == "motion" then

      7 local M = {
      7   copy_sync = {
            -- enables copy sync. by default, all registers are synchronized.
            -- to control which registers are synced, see the `sync_*` options.
            enable = false,

            -- ignore specific tmux buffers e.g. buffer0 = true to ignore the
            -- first buffer or named_buffer_name = true to ignore a named tmux
            -- buffer with name named_buffer_name :)
      7     ignore_buffers = { empty = false },

            -- TMUX >= 3.2: all yanks (and deletes) will get redirected to system
            -- clipboard by tmux
            redirect_to_clipboard = false,

            -- offset controls where register sync starts
            -- e.g. offset 2 lets registers 0 and 1 untouched
            register_offset = 0,

            -- overwrites vim.g.clipboard to redirect * and + to the system
            -- clipboard using tmux. If you sync your system clipboard without tmux,
            -- disable this option!
            sync_clipboard = true,

            -- synchronizes registers *, +, unnamed, and 0 till 9 with tmux buffers.
            sync_registers = true,

            -- synchronizes registers when pressing p and P.
            sync_registers_keymap_put = true,

            -- synchronizes registers when pressing (C-r) and ".
            sync_registers_keymap_reg = true,

            -- syncs deletes with tmux clipboard as well, it is adviced to
            -- do so. Nvim does not allow syncing registers 0 and 1 without
            -- overwriting the unnamed register. Thus, ddp would not be possible.
            sync_deletes = true,

            -- syncs the unnamed register with the first buffer entry from tmux.
            sync_unnamed = true,
      7   },
      7   navigation = {
            -- cycles to opposite pane while navigating into the border
            cycle_navigation = true,

            -- enables default keybindings (C-hjkl) for normal mode
            default_keymaps = false,

            -- prevents unzoom tmux when navigating beyond vim border
            persist_zoom = false,
      7   },
      7   resize = {
            -- enables default keybindings (A-hjkl) for normal mode
            default_keymaps = false,

            -- sets resize steps for x axis
            step_x = 1,

            -- sets resize steps for y axis
            step_y = 1,

            -- sets large resize steps for x axis
            large_x = 3,

            -- sets large resize steps for y axis

lua/unicode/names.lua

      5 local M = {}

        -- Special Unicode ranges that aren't in UnicodeData.txt individually
      5 local ranges = {
      5   { 0x3400, 0x4DB5, "Ideograph Extension A" },
      5   { 0x4E00, 0x9FFF, "CJK Ideograph" },
      5   { 0xAC00, 0xD7AF, "Hangul Syllable" },
      5   { 0xD800, 0xDB7F, "Non-Private Use High Surrogates" },
      5   { 0xDB80, 0xDBFF, "Private Use High Surrogates" },
      5   { 0xDC00, 0xDFFF, "Low Surrogates" },
      5   { 0xE000, 0xF8FF, "Private Use Zone" },
      5   { 0xFDD0, 0xFDEF, "<No Character>" },
      5   { 0x17000, 0x187EC, "Tangut Ideograph" },
      5   { 0x20000, 0x2A6D6, "Ideograph Extension B" },
      5   { 0x2A700, 0x2B73F, "Ideograph Extension C" },
      5   { 0xF0000, 0xFFFFD, "Character from Plane 15 for private use" },
      5   { 0x100000, 0x10FFFD, "Character from Plane 16 for private use" },
        }

        -- Exact codepoints with special names
      5 local exact = {
          [0xFFFE] = "<No Character (BOM)>",
          [0xFFFF] = "<No Character>",
        }

        -- Plane boundaries that are <No Character>
      5 local no_char_boundaries = {
          0x1FFFE, 0x1FFFF, 0x2FFFE, 0x2FFFF, 0x3FFFE, 0x3FFFF,
          0x4FFFE, 0x4FFFF, 0x5FFFE, 0x5FFFF, 0x6FFFE, 0x6FFFF,
          0x7FFFE, 0x7FFFF, 0x8FFFE, 0x8FFFF, 0x9FFFE, 0x9FFFF,
          0xAFFFE, 0xAFFFF, 0xBFFFE, 0xBFFFF, 0xCFFFE, 0xCFFFF,
          0xDFFFE, 0xDFFFF, 0xEFFFE, 0xEFFFF, 0xFFFFE, 0xFFFFF,
          0x10FFFE, 0x10FFFF,
        }

      5 local no_char_set = {}
    165 for _, v in ipairs(no_char_boundaries) do
    160   no_char_set[v] = true
        end

        --- Resolve a Unicode name for a decimal codepoint, using special range fallbacks.
        --- @param dec number Decimal codepoint value
        --- @param uni_dict table|nil The loaded unicode dictionary (decimal -> name)
        --- @return string
      5 function M.resolve(dec, uni_dict)
     30   if uni_dict then
     28     local name = uni_dict[dec]
     28     if name and name ~= "" then
      5       return name
            end
          end

     25   if exact[dec] then
      2     return exact[dec]
          end

     23   if no_char_set[dec] then
      2     return "<No Character>"
          end

    142   for _, range in ipairs(ranges) do
    140     if dec >= range[1] and dec <= range[2] then
     19       return range[3]
            end
          end

      2   return "Character not found"
        end

      5 return M

        --- Find digraphs matching a pattern.
        --- @param match string Search pattern (character, name, or decimal number)

      3 function M.find_digraph_by(match)

        -- Replacement for the deprecated nvim_buf_add_highlight(), built on extmarks.
        -- Keeps the old contract: col_end -1 highlights to end of line, ns_id 0/-1 is
        -- accepted (mapped to a shared namespace), out-of-range columns are clamped
        -- instead of raising, and the namespace id is returned.

      1 local M = {}

        local fallback_ns

        ---@param buf integer
        ---@param ns_id integer

      1 local s_true_map = {

          ["true"] = true,
          ["TRUE"] = true,

          ["YES"] = true,

      1 local s_false_map = {
          ["0"] = false,
          ["f"] = false,
          ["F"] = false,
          ["false"] = false,
          ["FALSE"] = false,
          ["False"] = false,
          ["NO"] = false,
          ["no"] = false,
          ["n"] = false,
        }

      1 local s_type_map = {
          boolean = function(val)
******0     if s_true_map[val] == true then
******0       return true
******0     elseif s_false_map[val] == false then
******0       return false
            end

******0     return nil
          end,
          number = function(val)
******0     return tonumber(val)
          end,
          string = function(val)
******0     return tostring(val)
          end,
          string_list = function(val)
******0     assert(type(val) == "string")
******0     return tostring(val)
          end,
          number_list = function(val)
******0     assert(type(val) == "string")
******0     return tostring(val)
          end,
        }

      1 function M.convert(value, type_name)
******0   local converter = s_type_map[type_name]
******0   if converter == nil then
******0     log.error("typing", "Cannot find a converter for `" .. value .. "` to " .. type_name .. ".")
          end

******0   local converted_value = converter(value)

******0   if converted_value == nil then
******0     log.error("typing", "Cannot convert `" .. value .. "` to " .. type_name .. ".")
          end

******0   return converted_value
        end

******0     return false

        -- opt("diffopt", "internal,filler,closeoff,linematch:60,algorithm:histogram,indent-heuristic")
        -- filler           Show filler lines, to keep the text synchronized with a window that has inserted lines at the same position. Mostly useful when windows are side-by-side and 'scrollbind' is set.
        -- context:{n}      Use a context of {n} lines between a change and a fold that contains unchanged lines.
        -- iblank           Ignore changes where lines are all blank. Adds the "-B" flag to the "diff" command if 'diffexpr' is empty.
        -- icase            Ignore changes in case of text. "a" and "A" are considered the same. Adds the "-i" flag to the "diff" command if 'diffexpr' is empty.
        -- iwhite           Ignore changes in amount of white space. Adds the "-b" flag to the "diff" command if 'diffexpr' is empty
        -- iwhiteall        Ignore all white space changes. Adds the "-w" flag to the "diff" command if 'diffexpr' is empty
        -- iwhiteeol        Ignore white space changes at end of line. Adds the "-Z" flag to the "diff" command if 'diffexpr' is empty.
        -- horizontal       Start diff mode with horizontal splits (unless explicitly specified otherwise).
        -- vertical         Start diff mode with vertical splits (unless explicitly specified otherwise).
        -- closeoff         When a window is closed where 'diff' is set and there is only one window remaining in the same tab page with 'diff' set, execute `:diffoff` in that window. This undoes a `:diffsplit` command.
        -- hiddenoff        Do not use diff mode for a buffer when it becomes hidden.
        -- foldcolumn:{n}   Set the 'foldcolumn' option to {n} when starting diff mode. Without this 2 is used.
        -- followwrap       Follow the 'wrap' option and leave as it is.
        -- internal         Use the internal diff library.
        -- indent-heuristic Use the indent heuristic for the internal diff library.
        -- linematch:{n}    Enable a second stage diff on each generated hunk in order to align lines. When the total number of lines in a hunk exceeds {n},
        --     the second stage diff will not be performed as very large hunks can cause noticeable lag. A recommended setting is "linematch:60", as this
        --     will enable alignment for a 2 buffer diff with hunks of up to 30 lines each, or a 3 buffer diff with hunks of up to 20 lines each.
        -- algorithm:{text} Use the specified diff algorithm with the internal diff engine. Currently supported algorithms are:
        --  myers      the default algorithm
        --  minimal    spend extra time to generate the smallest possible diff
        --  patience   patience diff algorithm
        --  histogram  histogram diff algorithm
